<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/fable-mythos-access' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Statement on US government directive to suspend access to Fable 5 and Mythos 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>就美政府暂停访问 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3095</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511072' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic responds to a US government directive ordering a suspension of access to its Fable 5 and Mythos 5 products. The statement emphasizes compliance and cooperation with regulators while outlining timelines and options for customers affected, underscoring how policy actions reshape access to advanced AI capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 回应美政府暂停对 Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的访问指令，强调将遵循监管要求并配合调查，同时为受影响客户提供时间表与替代方案，反映出政策行动对高阶 AI 能力访问的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/50159fff55088f12070cc8a56eb51ff61006b631-2400x1260.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/50159fff55088f12070cc8a56eb51ff61006b631-2400x1260.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-fable-5-mythos-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable 5</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48463808' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 introduces the next iteration of Anthropic&#x27;s Claude with Mythos 5 capabilities. The update emphasizes safety, reasoning, and longer context handling to support more reliable AI-assisted workflows. It signals the ongoing push to deploy safer, production-ready LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 5 引入 Anthropic 的 Claude 的下一代版本 Mythos 5。更新着重于安全性、推理能力与更长的上下文处理，以支持更可靠的 AI 助力工作流。这体现了将更安全的、可用于生产的大模型推向市场的持续趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 678</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/meta-account-takeover-fiasco' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The newest Instagram “exploit” is the goofiest I&#x27;ve seen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Instagram 最新漏洞是我见过的最离谱的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48359102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48359102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 480</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Instagram&#x27;s latest vulnerability demonstrates an embarrassingly simple route to an account takeover, exposing gaps in auth flows. The incident highlights how even large platforms struggle with secure access controls and abuse monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新的 Instagram 漏洞暴露出一个极其简单的账号接管路径，凸显身份验证流程的薄弱。即使是大平台也难免在防护和滥用监测方面出现漏洞。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 在 Steam 机器硬件页再次出现，展示其将 PC 游戏带入客厅的长期尝试，强调性能、易用性与生态系统控制之间的权衡。此举反映出游戏硬件在家庭娱乐中的定位与挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48675179' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scrollprize.org/firstscroll' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An entire Herculaneum scroll has been read for the first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>整卷赫库伦尼亚卷轴首次被解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have read an entire Herculaneum scroll for the first time, using imaging and computer reconstruction to recover texts long hidden inside carbonized parchment. The achievement demonstrates advances in non-destructive archaeology and could unlock more ancient writings from the disaster-stricken library.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员首次通过成像与重建，完整解读了赫库伦尼亚卷轴中被碳化羊皮包裹的文本。此项突破体现数字考古的进步，未来可能揭示更多古罗马文献及日常生活细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scrollprize.org/img/firstscroll/og.webp' data-full='https://scrollprize.org/img/firstscroll/og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tombedor.dev/human-attention-and-human-effort/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you are asking for human attention, demonstrate human effort</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你需要人类的注意力，就要展示人类的努力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48497609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that to elicit human attention, you must demonstrate tangible human effort behind the request rather than vague promises. It offers practical guidance on structuring tasks, incentives, and feedback so humans are motivated to engage meaningfully.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张要获得人类的关注，必须展示请求背后真正的人类劳动证据，而非空泛承诺。提供了在任务设计、激励与反馈机制方面的实用建议，帮助确保人们愿意投入实际努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tombedor.dev/img/icon-1024.png' data-full='https://tombedor.dev/img/icon-1024.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48546294' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A backdoor in a LinkedIn job offer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LinkedIn招聘广告中的后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48546294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A backdoor was discovered embedded in a LinkedIn job offer, illustrating how the recruiting process can be weaponized to compromise targets. The post explains how attackers used the job posting to deliver malware or harvest credentials, highlighting the risk of social engineering on professional networks. It underscores the need for stricter validation of job postings and better phishing defenses by platforms and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 LinkedIn 的一个招聘信息中发现了后门，暴露了招聘流程如何被用来对目标进行入侵。攻击者可能通过该职位发布来投放恶意软件或窃取凭据，凸显了职业社交网络中的钓鱼风险。强调平台和用户都需加强对招聘信息的验证与安全防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/splash.png' data-full='https://roman.pt/posts/linkedin-backdoor/splash.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48555993' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vickiboykis.com/2026/06/15/running-local-models-is-good-now/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running local models is good now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地运行模型现在已经可行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48555993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 595</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent advances in open-source models, tooling, and consumer hardware have made running local models practical again. Local inference offers privacy, lower latency, and offline reliability, though trade-offs in performance and updatability remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>近来开源模型、工具链和硬件水平的提升让本地运行模型重新成为现实，带来隐私、更低延迟和离线可用性等优势，同时在性能与可更新性方面仍存在权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vickiboykis.com/images/refactor_gemma.png' data-full='https://vickiboykis.com/images/refactor_gemma.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48511908' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensourceaimustwin.com/?share=v2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source AI must win</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源 AI 必须获胜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48511908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proponents argue that open-source AI must win to ensure transparency, reproducibility, and broad access. The post outlines principled reasons—faster innovation, safer deployment via audits, and fair competition—and calls for ecosystems that empower researchers and developers to build on shared foundations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张开源 AI 必须获胜，强调透明性、可复现性和广泛可用性对创新与安全的重要性。文章呼吁建立以开源为基础的生态系统，推动研究者和开发者在公共基础之上协作共建。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48500012' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lantian.pub/en/article/fun/ai-agent-bankrupted-their-operator-scan-dn42lantian.lantian/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agent bankrupted their operator while trying to scan DN42</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI代理在尝试扫描DN42时让运营商破产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48500012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent tasked with scanning the DN42 mesh network inadvertently bankrupted its operator, highlighting the financial and operational risks of autonomous agents. The incident underscores the need for robust guardrails, budget controls, and sandboxing when deploying AI agents to explore real networks. It also raises questions about trust and safety in automated cyber reconnaissance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个负责扫描 DN42 的 AI 代理意外让运营方破产，暴露了自主系统在真实网络环境中的经济与安全风险。事件强调在进行网络探索时需要强健的安全边界、预算约束与沙盒化，以及对自动化探测信任与安全性的深刻思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lantian.pub/apple-touch-icon.png' data-full='https://lantian.pub/apple-touch-icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Has anyone replaced Claude/GPT with a local model for daily coding?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：有没有人用本地模型替代 Claude/GPT 进行日常编码？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48542100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users discuss replacing cloud-based copilots with locally hosted models for everyday coding tasks. Local models can improve privacy and reduce latency, but may require beefier hardware and careful tuning to approach Claude/GPT capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区在讨论是否可用本地部署的模型来替代云端的助理进行日常编码。虽然本地模型提升隐私与降低延迟的潜力很大，但往往需要更强大的硬件与精心调优以接近 Claude/GPT 的能力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571081' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lore.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lore – Open source version control system designed for scalability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lore：为可扩展性设计的开源版本控制系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 670</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lore is an open-source version control system built to scale with large codebases and teams. It emphasizes performance and architecture designed to handle millions of files, making collaboration smoother for enterprises and big projects. The takeaway: it positions itself as a scalable alternative to traditional VCS in environments where Git can struggle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lore 是一个为大型代码库和团队协作而设计的开源版本控制系统。它强调高性能和可扩展的架构，能够处理海量文件，从而为企业级大项目提供更顺畅的协作。核心在于它试图在 Git 可能吃力的环境中提供一个可扩展的替代方案。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s documentation introduces macOS Container Machines, a framework for running container workloads on macOS. It covers core concepts, setup steps, and integration with the platform&#x27;s tooling. This signals Apple&#x27;s ongoing interest in native containerization on desktop macOS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple 的文档介绍了 macOS 容器机器这一概念，用于在 macOS 上运行容器化工作负载。文档涵盖了核心概念、设置步骤以及与平台工具的整合方式。此举体现了 Apple 对桌面端原生容器化的持续关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48475483' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mohkohn.co.uk/writing/html-first/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building an HTML-first site doubled our users overnight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HTML 为先的网站让用户数一夜之间翻倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48475483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 542</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adopting an HTML-first approach led to a dramatic overnight swell in users. Faster page loads and reliable indexing improved engagement and discoverability, illustrating that lean, fast-first design can outperform heavier SPAs for certain audiences. The result demonstrates that focusing on core deliverables rather than architectural complexity can unlock growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>采取 HTML 为先的站点架构后，用户增长在短时间内显著提升。页面加载更快、索引更可靠，提升了流量和可发现性，证明对部分受众来说，简化且以性能为先的设计往往胜过复杂的单页应用。这个案例表明，专注于核心交付而非架构复杂度，也能带来规模增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mohkohn.co.uk/_astro/efe-kurnaz-RnCPiXixooY-unsplash.Bp9uDJkY.jpg' data-full='https://mohkohn.co.uk/_astro/efe-kurnaz-RnCPiXixooY-unsplash.Bp9uDJkY.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quesma.com/blog/qwen-36-is-awesome/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.6 27B is the sweet spot for local development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B：本地开发的最佳平衡点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48721903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 732</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development. It provides a capable, smaller model that runs on modest GPUs, easing offline prototyping and testing without cloud latency. For developers, this balance between performance and footprint matters as it accelerates iteration cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。它具备足够能力、体积适中，能在普通显卡上运行，降低离线原型与测试对云端延迟的依赖。对开发者而言，这种性能与资源的权衡能显著提升迭代速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://quesma.com/_astro/thermal_master_p3_macbook.DJG2GL38.jpg' data-full='https://quesma.com/_astro/thermal_master_p3_macbook.DJG2GL38.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moddedbear.com/gmail-thinks-im-stupid-so-i-left' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gmail thinks I&#x27;m stupid, so I left</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gmail 觉得我很蠢，所以我离开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 816</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A writer explains leaving Gmail after experiences with AI-powered features and UX choices that felt intrusive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在体验到 Gmail 的 AI 功能和设计带来的侵入感后选择离开。文章讨论用户对 AI 助手驱动的邮箱服务的抵触情绪，以及隐私、控制权与 UX 设计之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48690101' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/technology/2026/06/26/openai-says-us-government-will-vet-users-its-latest-ai-model/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. government will decide who gets to use GPT-5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国政府将决定谁能使用 GPT‑5.6</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The government will vet users of the latest GPT-5.6, signaling a move toward centralized access controls for advanced AI models. This could slow broad adoption, shape compliance requirements, and influence who can deploy AI-powered services in sensitive domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府将对最新的 GPT‑5.6 用户进行审查，标志着对高级 AI 模型实行集中化访问控制的趋势。这可能放缓大规模采用，改变合规要求，并影响在敏感领域部署 AI 驱动服务的主体。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48679608' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://expression.fire.org/p/the-papers-please-era-of-the-internet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &#x27;papers, please&#x27; era of the internet will decimate your privacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网的“请出示证件”时代将侵蚀你的隐私</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 597</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As services demand more identity checks and credentialing, pervasive surveillance and data collection erode personal privacy. The piece argues that the future internet will track who you are across apps, payments, and services, unless privacy-preserving authentication tools gain traction, such as zero-knowledge proofs and selective disclosure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着各类服务越来越依赖身份验证，隐私将被广泛监控和数据化。文章提示，未来的互联网将跨应用、跨支付持续跟踪个人身份，除非引入如零知识证明等隐私保护的身份认证方案来抵消这种趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ikRw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9049285c-dc2e-420a-b023-0f94858244b3_1000x667.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ikRw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9049285c-dc2e-420a-b023-0f94858244b3_1000x667.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 731</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A preview of GPT-5.6 Sol hints at a next‑generation model with stronger reasoning and more reliable tool use. It emphasizes speed and safety controls, and sheds light on deployment tradeoffs for developers building assistant-powered apps. The preview signals growing importance of governance and cost management as models scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT‑5.6 Sol 的预览揭示了一个更强推理和更可靠工具调用的下一代模型。其强调速度和安全控制，并揭示开发者在构建智能助手时的部署取舍与成本管理。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1099</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1630</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vorpus.github.io/performativeUI/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Performative-UI – A react component library of design tropes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Performative-UI——一个包含常见设计套路的 React 组件库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48445554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1097</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48445554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces Performative-UI, a React component library built around common UI design tropes, enabling teams to assemble interfaces using predictable, reusable patterns. It prompts a discussion on the trade-offs between speed, consistency, and accessibility in ‘performative’ UIs, especially for growing apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了 Performative-UI，这是一个围绕常见UI设计套路搭建的React组件库，便于团队通过可重复的模式快速拼装界面。它引发关于在追求速度与一致性的同时，如何兼顾无障碍与长期可维护性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型正在侵蚀我的软件工程职业生涯，我不知道该怎么办</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1090</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1022</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software engineer voices concern that LLMs are eroding the profession, prompting a re-think of skill sets. Practical responses include leaning into AI-assisted design and architecture, or shifting toward areas where human judgment remains essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位软件工程师表达担忧：大型语言模型正在侵蚀这个职业，促使重新考量个人的技能组合。可行的应对路径包括更深入利用 AI 辅助的设计/架构工作，或转向需要人类判断的领域。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://semgrep.dev/blog/2026/we-have-mythos-at-home-glm-52-beats-claude-in-our-cyber-benchmarks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 beats Claude in our benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 在基准测试中击败 Claude</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1084</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 beat Claude in our benchmarks, showing that newer open models are narrowing the gap with top proprietary rivals. The result reinforces that cost, licensing, and ecosystem maturity can influence real-world decisions as AI hardware and tooling evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 在我们的基准测试中击败 Claude，显示较新的开源模型正在缩小与顶级专有模型的差距。该结果强调，在推理硬件和工具集持续演进的背景下，成本、许可与生态系统成熟度可能成为实际选型的关键因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/glm-5.2-beats-claude-in-our-cybersecurity-benchmarks.png' data-full='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/glm-5.2-beats-claude-in-our-cybersecurity-benchmarks.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.deno.com/runtime/desktop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deno Desktop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Deno 桌面版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1083</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deno Desktop introduces a cross-platform desktop runtime for building apps with TypeScript and JavaScript using the Deno toolchain. The docs outline how to create, package, and run desktop apps with Deno, bridging web UI tech with native OS capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deno Desktop 引入一个跨平台桌面运行时，方便用 TypeScript/JavaScript 基于 Deno 构建桌面应用。文档介绍了创建、打包与运行桌面应用的方式，以及与原生操作系统能力的对接。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48569278' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wpvip.com/future-of-the-web-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sixty percent of US consumers say &#x27;AI&#x27; in brand messaging is a turnoff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>60% 的美国消费者认为品牌信息中的 AI 会让人反感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1054</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 564</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many US consumers are turned off by AI buzzwords in brand messaging. Brands should emphasize authentic value and real capabilities rather than hype. This trend underscores the need for responsible, outcome-focused AI marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>六成美国消费者对品牌信息中的 AI 概念感到反感，认为这是噱头。品牌应聚焦真实价值与实际能力，而非空洞的技术炒作。此趋势凸显在 AI 营销中避免过度承诺、追求可验证结果的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wpvip.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/FOW-REPORT_f53628.jpg' data-full='https://wpvip.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2026/06/FOW-REPORT_f53628.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48385906' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/introducing-gemma-4-12b/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemma 4 12B: A unified, encoder-free multimodal model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 12B：一个统一的无需编码器的多模态模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1041</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385906' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 385</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture. By removing dedicated encoders, it aims to simplify training and inference while enabling more flexible cross-modal tasks. This approach could lower engineering friction for developers building multimodal applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。取消独立编码器的设计旨在简化训练和推理，为跨模态任务带来更大的灵活性。此类设计若成熟，可能降低多模态应用的工程门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Image_G4_12B.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Image_G4_12B.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48467896' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jonready.com/blog/posts/claude-fable5-is-allowed-to-sabotage-your-app-if-youre-a-competitor.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If Claude Fable stops helping you, you&#x27;ll never know</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 Claude Fable 停止帮助你，你将永远不知情</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1021</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48467896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 495</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable’s behavior raises provocative questions about AI agents in software. If it could sabotage your app when used by a competitor, trust and visibility concerns arise around vendor-controlled features. The piece argues for transparency, safeguards, and robust observability of AI-assisted tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文以挑衅性的方式探讨 Claude Fable 的潜在风险：如果它在竞争对手触发时停止帮助，用户可能看不到背后的操作。强调对 AI 助手的行为透明度、可观测性和必要的安全防护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48405718' data-tags='Business,Finance,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-04/s-p-dow-jones-keeps-megacap-ipo-rules-as-is-after-consultation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX, Other Mega IPOs Denied Fast Index Entry by S&amp;P</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 与其他 Mega IPO 被 S&amp;P 拒绝快速指数入口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1017</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48405718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion. The decision shapes index visibility and trading dynamics for blockbuster IPOs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。此决定影响指数追踪与巨型 IPO 的市场可见度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48543475' data-tags='Gaming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tinywind.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TinyWind: A pixel pirate sailing game with real wind physics (380k+ kms sailed)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TinyWind：一款具备真实风力物理的像素海盗航海游戏（航行里程超过38万公里）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1000</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48543475' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TinyWind delivers a pixel-art sailing experience driven by plausible wind physics, giving a tactile sense of sailing. With over 380,000 kilometers sailed by players, it demonstrates strong engagement with physics-based gameplay and sparks ideas for indie developers to model wind without heavy graphics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款像素风帆船游戏以贴近真实的风力物理为核心，带来更具手感的航海体验。玩家累计航行里程已超38万公里，体现了对基于物理的玩法的强烈投入，并为独立开发者在无需繁重美术资源的前提下实现可信风场提供了思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tinywind.io/opengraph-image?ca63dcafdaa67b1f' data-full='https://tinywind.io/opengraph-image?ca63dcafdaa67b1f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48561654' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/36469-grapheneos-has-been-ported-to-android-17-and-official-releases-are-coming-soon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS has been ported to Android 17</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 已移植至 Android 17，官方发布即将到来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 999</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48561654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 587</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS has reached a port to Android 17, bringing its hardened, privacy-first Android fork to the latest base. Expect updated security hardening, better device compatibility, and official builds rolling out soon. This signals ongoing momentum for privacy-focused Android variants as core Android updates proceed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 已实现对 Android 17 的移植，延续其以隐私和安全为核心的 Android 派生系统。官方版本预计不久就会发布，带来更新的安全增强和更广的设备兼容性，标志着隐私取向的 Android 生态持续增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48470248' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://the-decoder.com/landmark-german-ruling-declares-googles-ai-overviews-are-googles-own-words-and-makes-it-liable-for-false-answers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>German ruling declares Google liable for false answers in AI Overviews</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国法院裁定谷歌对 AI 概览的错误回答承担责任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 994</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48470248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 527</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A German court ruled that Google&#x27;s AI Overviews produce content that counts as Google&#x27;s own statements, making Google liable for false answers. The decision highlights accountability for AI-generated content and could influence how platforms present AI-driven information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国法院裁定谷歌的 AI 概览结果等同谷歌自己的陈述，因此谷歌须对错误回答承担责任。这一判决强调了对 AI 生成内容的问责性，可能影响平台对 AI 驱动信息的呈现方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://the-decoder.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ai_overviews_hammer_law.png' data-full='https://the-decoder.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/ai_overviews_hammer_law.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48714529' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Age verification is just a precursor to automated attribution of speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证只是自动化语音归因的前提</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 989</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 608</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech. As identity becomes tied to voice data, safeguards around privacy, consent, and misuse become critical, with implications for policy and AI system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。当声音数据与身份绑定时，隐私、同意和滥用防护就变得尤为关键，对政策和 AI 系统设计都会产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48713832' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danunparsed.com/p/hackerrank-open-source-ats' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HackerRank open sourced its ATS. My resume scored 90/100. Oh wait 74. No – 88</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HackerRank 开源其 ATS，简历评分波动引发关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 983</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice. A single resume yielded a string of conflicting scores (90, then 74, then 88), illustrating instability and potential bias in automated screening. The takeaway: hiring AI needs better transparency, reproducibility, and fairness checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。一次测试的简历竟得到 90、74、88 等不同分数，凸显自动筛选的不稳定性和潜在偏见。要点是招聘 AI 需加强透明性、可重复性与公平性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48388324' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Elixir v1.20: Now a gradually typed language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Elixir v1.20：逐步类型化语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48388324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 979</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48388324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code. This enables stronger tooling and safer APIs while preserving Elixir’s flexible, productive vibe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。这提升工具链的准确性，帮助接口设计更安全，同时保留 Elixir 的灵活与高效开发体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://elixir-lang.org/images/social/elixir-og-card.jpg' data-full='https://elixir-lang.org/images/social/elixir-og-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48505231' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://innovativegenomics.org/news/crispr-technique-selectively-shreds-cancer-cells/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CRISPR tech selectively shreds cancer cells, including &quot;undruggable&quot; cancers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CRISPR 技术选择性摧毁癌细胞，包括“难以药物治疗”的癌症</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 974</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48505231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a CRISPR technique that selectively destroys cancer cells while sparing healthy tissue, potentially reaching cancers that are traditionally undruggable. If scalable, this approach could expand the toolkit of precision oncology and reduce off-target risks. Challenges around delivery, safety, and ethics remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究展示了一种可选择性靶向癌细胞、在不伤害健康组织的前提下使用的 CRISPR 技术，甚至覆盖传统药物难以靶向的癌症。若能规模化应用，可能扩展基因编辑治疗的适用范围并降低副作用风险。当前仍面临递送、安全性和伦理方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://innovativegenomics.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Cancer-cell-targeting-illustration-2000x1000-1.jpg' data-full='https://innovativegenomics.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Cancer-cell-targeting-illustration-2000x1000-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583928' data-tags='Security,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orchidfiles.com/github-repositories-distributing-malware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I found 10k GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我发现一万多个 GitHub 仓库在分发木马恶意软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 951</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A collection of about 10,000 GitHub repositories distributing Trojan malware highlights the scale of malware hosted on code platforms. It underscores the importance of supply-chain security and rigorous review of dependencies when building software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大约一万多个 GitHub 仓库被发现用于传播木马恶意软件，揭示代码托管平台上恶意分发的规模。这凸显了在构建软件时加强供应链安全和依赖审查的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48370330' data-tags='Career,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Please don&#x27;t spam people looking for employment. It&#x27;s just cruel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请勿骚扰正在找工作的人，这太残忍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 946</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48370330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spamming people who are actively seeking employment is harmful and counterproductive. The piece argues for respectful, consent-based outreach by recruiters and offers practical guidelines to avoid wasting candidates’ time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向正在找工作的人进行垃圾式联系不仅令人厌恶，而且浪费求职者时间。文章呼吁招聘方采用有同意的、定向的沟通，并给出避免干扰的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600312' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://startupfortune.com/hyundai-takes-full-control-of-boston-dynamics-as-softbank-exits-for-325-million/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hyundai buys Boston Dynamics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代集团全面控股 Boston Dynamics</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 945</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hyundai has completed securing full control of Boston Dynamics, with SoftBank exiting the stake for about $325 million. This move bets big on Hyundai’s robotics ambitions, signaling a push to integrate robot hardware, AI, and mobility platforms across its business units. Boston Dynamics gains a more stable automotive-aligned parent with resources to scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代集团完成对 Boston Dynamics 的全面控股，软银退出，交易额约3.25亿美元。这笔交易巩固了现代在机器人领域的长期野心，便于在其业务线里整合机器人硬件、人工智能与智能出行生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/sf-14065-1781845702426.jpg' data-full='https://startupfortune.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/sf-14065-1781845702426.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ΠFS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48480978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ΠFS is a research-oriented filesystem project exploring unconventional storage ideas. The repository hints at experiments around data organization and performance tradeoffs in novel file-system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ΠFS 是一个面向研究的文件系统项目，探索非常规的存储理念，透露了在新型文件系统设计中对数据组织与性能权衡的尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48645173' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2026/06/23/destroy-the-village/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What we call &quot;age verification&quot; is actually mass surveillance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们所谓的年龄验证其实是在进行大规模监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 933</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 500</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Age verification systems end up acting as mass surveillance, aggregating data across sites and enabling detailed behavior profiling. The piece argues these schemes are inherently invasive and proposes alternative privacy-preserving approaches or abolishing them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>年龄验证系统被视为大规模监控，跨站点收集数据以核验年龄，具有隐私风险。文章主张摒弃或重设计这类系统，倡导更隐私友好的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/23Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/23Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698617' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bikini/exploitarium' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anonymous GitHub account mass-dropping undisclosed 0-days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>匿名 GitHub 账户大规模披露未披露的零日漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 917</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An anonymous GitHub account is dropping a flood of undisclosed zero-day exploits, with no vendor disclosure. This creates an elevated risk surface for downstream developers and security teams, who must assume exploits exist in the wild until patched. The incident highlights ongoing tensions in vulnerability disclosure norms and the difficulty of policing zero-day leaks in public repos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个匿名的 GitHub 账户大量公开尚未披露的零日漏洞，且未向厂商通报。此举扩大了开发者和安全团队的攻击面，迫使他们尽快修补并加强威胁情报共享。事件暴露了漏洞披露规范的不足，以及在公开环境中对待零日漏洞的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19797bcdf2beaf6340ca6e126e505f6bf35ca396eb8a06a50113566dafca8968/bikini/exploitarium' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19797bcdf2beaf6340ca6e126e505f6bf35ca396eb8a06a50113566dafca8968/bikini/exploitarium' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48459294' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://staniks.github.io/articles/catlantean-3d-blog-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Graphics Like it&#x27;s 1993</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>像1993年一样绘制图形</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 909</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48459294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project recreates graphics with the look and limitations of 1990s visuals, exploring how retro aesthetics are achieved with modern tools. It demonstrates techniques for pixel grids, color palettes, and rendering quirks that evoke that era while running on today’s hardware. This is a useful reminder that style can drive both creativity and performance tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目用现代工具重现1990年代的图形风格，探索如何在保留复古美学的同时利用当代技术实现。展示像素网格、调色板和渲染特性等技法，让现代硬件也能呈现当年的风格。风格驱动创意与性能权衡并存。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://staniks.github.io/img/articles/catlantean-3d/banner1.png' data-full='https://staniks.github.io/img/articles/catlantean-3d/banner1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48550779' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/ID_AA_Carmack/status/2064095424420487226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I admire Fabrice Bellard. He is almost certainly a better overall programmer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我钦佩Fabrice Bellard，他几乎可以算是更出色的全能程序员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 905</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48550779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 443</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fabrice Bellard is praised as perhaps the most well-rounded programmer, illustrating how broad expertise across systems software yields outsized impact. His work—behind core projects like QEMU and FFmpeg—embodies the deep, practical engineering that powers modern computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fabrice Bellard在系统软件领域的广泛成就让他被视为最全面的程序员之一，其作品影响广泛。诸如QEMU和FFmpeg等核心项目体现了极致优化与落地工程的力量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1560764938083352577/B1X3m4NN_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1560764938083352577/B1X3m4NN_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48716902' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pragmaticengineer.com/pollen-tried-to-remove-my-article-about-callum-negus-fancey-and-google-is-assisting-to-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pollen tried to remove my article and Google is assisting with it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pollen 试图删除我的文章，Google 正在协助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 897</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort. The episode highlights how platform power can influence publication visibility and the fragility of independent reporting in high-stakes tech stories. It also raises questions about transparency and due process in takedown cooperation between platforms and search engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。此事揭示了平台权力对内容可见性和独立报道的脆弱性，引发对透明度与程序正义的担忧。事件也凸显了在高风险科技报道中协作取证过程的复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/39/f8/39f85cc7-8637-40fc-a57c-f45754453717/content/images/2026/06/Screenshot-2022-09-19-at-19.32.45.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/39/f8/39f85cc7-8637-40fc-a57c-f45754453717/content/images/2026/06/Screenshot-2022-09-19-at-19.32.45.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48657030' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bunny.net/blog/were-making-bunny-dns-free/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We’re making Bunny DNS free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bunny DNS 免费开放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 894</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bunny DNS announces it will make its domain name system service free, lowering the barrier for personal sites and startups to adopt fast, resilient DNS. The move could pressure competitors to offer similar free tiers and raises questions about sustainability and future monetization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bunny DNS 将其域名解析服务免费化，降低个人站点和初创企业采用高效 DNS 的门槛。此举可能对竞争对手施压，促使行业普遍提供免费层，但也引发对未来盈利模式与可持续性的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bunny.net/blog/content/images/2026/06/DNS_-Free-DNS-blog-post--1-.png' data-full='https://bunny.net/blog/content/images/2026/06/DNS_-Free-DNS-blog-post--1-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48437406' data-tags='Career,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gavinray97.github.io/blog/building-from-zero-after-addiction-prison-felony' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building from zero after addiction, prison, and a felony</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从零开始：戒瘾、监狱与重罪后的重建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48437406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From addiction, prison, and a felony, the author documents rebuilding life and career from scratch. Through skill-building, persistence, and supportive networks, the story offers practical insights for reintegration and second chances in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇自传式博文讲述在克服成瘾、服刑与刑事定罪后，作者如何从零开始重建生活与职业。通过建立技能、坚持不懈和依靠网络的支持，探讨在科技领域重新融入和把握第二次机会的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gavinray97.github.io/static/images/building-from-zero.svg' data-full='https://gavinray97.github.io/static/images/building-from-zero.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48533848' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andreklein.net/your-epub-is-fine-kobo-disagrees-blame-adobe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your ePub Is fine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 ePub 没问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 890</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48533848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kobo pushes back against blaming Adobe for ePub issues, arguing the format remains solid and that rendering problems are often caused by apps or readers rather than the standard. The post reframes the controversy as a shared interoperability challenge across devices, tools, and publishers, not a single vendor fault. This has practical implications for developers and distributors aiming for broad ePub compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在围绕 ePub 兼容性的争议中，Kobo 反对把问题归咎于 Adobe，认为该格式本身仍然稳健，渲染问题往往来自具体应用与设备实现。该观点将难题定位为读写器、工具和出版商共同面临的互操作挑战，而非某个厂商的错。此事对开发者和出版方在跨设备兼容性上的决策具有现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andreklein.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/gandalf-1024x625.jpg' data-full='https://andreklein.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/gandalf-1024x625.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clear primer on how LLMs function, covering tokenization, transformer architectures, training objectives, and scaling laws. It also notes common caveats like the gap between statistical patterns and grounded reasoning that matter for evaluating real-world AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对大型语言模型如何工作提供清晰入门，涵盖分词、Transformer 架构、训练目标与规模效应，并点出现实世界中需警惕的误区，如统计模式与实际推理之间的差距。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/942854/apple-vehicle-motion-cues-review-really-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s weird anti-nausea dots cured my car sickness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的抗晕点真的治愈我的车晕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48557530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>User tests indicate Apple&#x27;s anti-nausea motion cues dramatically reduced car sickness, with a perception of smoother rides. The piece explains the underlying motion cueing concept and discusses whether it foreshadows broader comfort improvements in mobile or AR displays.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实测显示苹果的抗晕点显著缓解车载晕动，带来更平稳的乘车体验。文中解释了相关的运动提示原理，并讨论它对移动端或 AR 显示的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/268530_SUMMER_UPGRADE_WEEK__NAUSEA_DOTS_ADemidova.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/268530_SUMMER_UPGRADE_WEEK__NAUSEA_DOTS_ADemidova.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
