<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48517377' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://desfontain.es/blog/banning-noise.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Noise infusion banned from statistical products published by Census Bureau</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人口普查局发布的统计产品禁止噪声注入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 868</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48517377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy-preserving data often relies on adding noise; this policy change bans noise infusion in published statistics, shifting how privacy is maintained. The move could affect the utility of released data and push researchers to alternative methods, such as synthetic data or different disclosure controls. Data practitioners should watch for downstream effects on accuracy and bias in released datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人口普查局宣布在对外发布的统计产品中不再应用噪声注入以保护隐私。此举改变了以往通过添加随机噪声实现差分隐私的做法，可能影响数据可用性和分析精度。研究人员和机构或需转向其他隐私保护方法，如合成数据或新的披露控制策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://desfontain.es/blog/images/banning-noise.png' data-full='https://desfontain.es/blog/images/banning-noise.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48471638' data-tags='Gaming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://screen.toys/firewood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firewood Splitting Simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>劈柴分裂模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 863</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48471638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lighthearted, physics-based simulator that lets you chop virtual firewood, delivering satisfying feedback loops and simple rules. It’s a tiny reminder that well-tuned mechanics can be more engaging than complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一款以物理为基础的趣味模拟器，玩家通过虚拟劈柴获得直观的反馈与上手简单的玩法。简洁的游戏机制往往比复杂性更具吸引力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48567759' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://artificialanalysis.ai/articles/glm-5-2-is-the-new-leading-open-weights-model-on-the-artificial-analysis-intelligence-index' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.2 is the new leading open weights model on Artificial Analysis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.2 成为 Artificial Analysis 上的开源权重新领先者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 862</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48567759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 has emerged as the leading open-weight model on the Artificial Analysis index, outperforming its predecessors in benchmark tests. It demonstrates strong generalization with competitive inference speed, illustrating how open-weight ecosystems continue to close the gap with closed models. This trend lowers barriers for researchers to experiment with powerful LLMs at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 已成为开源权重模型的领先者，领先于前代模型并在基准测试中表现突出。它在推理速度和泛化能力方面表现有竞争力，标志着开源权重生态的成熟。此趋势降低了研究人员在大模型实验中的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/204f6bd201e215286fd69d162a84dfdf4a24d197-768x768.webp?w=1200&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/6vfeftx9/articles/204f6bd201e215286fd69d162a84dfdf4a24d197-768x768.webp?w=1200&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48568058' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/americas-compact-between-science-and-politics-is-broken/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. science is in chaos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国科学界陷入混乱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 852</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48568058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1038</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis arguing the U.S. science-politics compact is broken, threatening long-term funding, independent oversight, and scientific consensus.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇分析指出，美国科学与政治之间的默契已破裂，长期资助、独立监督和科学共识受到威胁。文章强调政治极化和短期政策周期削弱了研究生态系统，也影响着科技创新的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/14de7018-3a11-43b8-8392-6e83ccd5b0a3/saw070826Roge01.jpg?m=1779983432.668&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/14de7018-3a11-43b8-8392-6e83ccd5b0a3/saw070826Roge01.jpg?m=1779983432.668&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ladybird.org/posts/changing-how-we-develop-ladybird/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Changing how we develop Ladybird</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 840</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48409191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 535</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post outlines a shift in how the Ladybird project is developed, focusing on new workflows, tooling, and contributor practices to accelerate iteration while maintaining quality. It explains the rationale behind changing development dynamics and what readers might expect in future releases. The change aims to improve collaboration, transparency, and build reliability for users and contributors alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文阐述对 Ladybird 项目开发方式的变革，聚焦新的工作流、工具链和贡献者实践，旨在在保持质量的同时加快迭代速度。文中解释了改变开发动力学的理由，以及未来版本释放中可能出现的变化。此举旨在提升协作、透明度和对用户及贡献者的可靠性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude includes an identity verification flow to control access for sensitive features and enterprise usage. The guide covers when verification is required, what methods are supported, and how it affects access privileges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 提供身份验证流程以控制对敏感功能和企业用途的访问。指南说明何时需要验证、支持哪些方法，以及对权限访问的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discourse on achieving pixel-perfect rendering across frames, weighing visual fidelity against performance in modern graphics pipelines. The piece offers practical perspectives on how engineers balance quality, predictability, and resource use to reach consistently polished results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于在每一帧中实现像素级完美渲染的讨论，权衡在现代图形管线中视觉保真度与性能的关系。文中提出工程师在质量、可预测性和资源使用之间的平衡策略，以实现持续一致的高质量输出。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/asia-pacific/apple-raises-prices-macbooks-ipads-memory-costs-skyrocket-2026-06-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple raises prices of MacBooks, iPads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果提高 MacBook 与 iPad 价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 830</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple raised prices on MacBooks and iPads, citing higher costs for memory and components. The move tightens premium device margins and could influence consumer purchasing behavior, particularly during upcoming refresh cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果因内存等组件成本上涨，提高了 MacBook 与 iPad 的定价。这一举措收窄了高端设备的利润空间，可能影响消费者在未来升级周期的购买决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48421858' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opencv.org/opencv-5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCV 5 Is Here: The Biggest Leap in Years for Computer Vision</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCV 5 来临：多年来计算机视觉的最大跃迁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 829</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48421858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCV 5 arrives with a major leap for computer vision, including API overhauls, performance gains, and expanded hardware and back-end support. It signals a more streamlined path to building, deploying, and optimizing CV apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCV 5 发布带来计算机视觉领域的重大跃迁，包含 API 大改、性能提升以及对新硬件和后端的扩展支持。此举为构建、部署与优化计算机视觉应用提供更简便的路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opencv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/opencv-5.png' data-full='https://opencv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/opencv-5.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48465675' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/06/09/ceos-who-think-ai-replaces-their-employees-are-just-bad-ceos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CEOs who think AI replaces their employees are just bad CEOs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认为 AI 会取代员工的 CEO 只是坏 CEO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 826</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48465675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 303</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is framed as a productivity enhancer, not a substitute for workers. The piece argues that CEOs who assume AI will replace employees misjudge the technology, underscoring the importance of reskilling and human-AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 AI 应该提升生产力而非替代员工。文章指出认为 AI 会取代员工的 CEO 常常误判技术，强调再培训和人机协作的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622867' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.newgas.net/did-my-old-job-only-exist-because-of-fraud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Did my old job only exist because of fraud?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的旧工作是不是只是因为欺诈而存在？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 822</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning whether some roles exist due to misaligned incentives or fraud, the piece explores the fragility of certain job markets and the potential for automation. It advocates for transparency, better verification, and data-driven evaluation of roles rather than anecdotal narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑某些岗位是否因激励错位或欺诈而存在，探讨了部分职业市场的脆弱性与自动化的潜在影响。倡导提高透明度、强化核验，并以数据驱动来评估岗位，而非仅凭主观叙述。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://david.newgas.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-4-1024x148.png' data-full='https://david.newgas.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-4-1024x148.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/AnthropicAI/status/2072106151890809341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Department of Commerce has lifted export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>商务部取消对 Claude Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的出口管制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 815</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution. The move could accelerate global AI deployment and competition, while inviting regulatory and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。这一举措可能加速全球 AI 的部署与竞争，同时带来监管与安全方面的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/24/openai-unveils-its-first-custom-chip-built-by-broadcom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI unveils its first custom chip, built by Broadcom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 发布首个定制芯片，由 Broadcom 打造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48663324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 813</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48663324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI has unveiled its first custom silicon, built in partnership with Broadcom. The move signals a shift toward owning more of the hardware stack to optimize model serving and efficiency, potentially reducing reliance on Nvidia GPUs. If successful, it could reshape cloud economics and push competitors to accelerate in-house hardware efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 公布了首款定制芯片，与 Broadcom 合作打造，旨在优化自家模型的推理与训练性能。此举标志对硬件环节更深度掌控，可能降低对外部供应商的依赖，并影响云端成本与定价竞争。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-2026-06-24-at-9.49.59-AM.jpg?resize=1200,670' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-2026-06-24-at-9.49.59-AM.jpg?resize=1200,670' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig&#x27;s ecosystem keeps growing as a further $400k is pledged to its software foundation. The donation signals continued community support and could accelerate language tooling, compiler work, and ecosystem initiatives that push Zig toward broader adoption and stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 生态系统再次获得资金支持，向其软件基金会再捐出40万美元，显示社区的持续支持。捐款有望推动语言工具链、编译器改进和生态项目，加速 Zig 的普及与稳定性提升。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/norway-imposes-near-ban-ai-elementary-school-2026-06-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Norway imposes near ban on AI in elementary school</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威对小学生使用AI几近全面禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 801</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 575</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norway is moving toward a near-ban on AI use in elementary schools, tightening rules on what students and teachers can access on school devices. The policy underscores safety concerns and data privacy while grappling with how to responsibly integrate AI into early education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挪威正推动对小学生在校使用AI的几近全面禁令，严格限制师生在校设备上的AI工具使用。此举凸显对安全与数据隐私的关注，以及在早教阶段如何负责任地引入AI的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bluewin.ch/en/news/switzerland/parliament-lifts-ban-on-new-nuclear-power-plants-3257535.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swiss parliament lifts ban on new nuclear power plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士议会解除对新核电厂的禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 799</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48585746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 944</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switzerland&#x27;s parliament voted to lift the ban on new nuclear power plants, signaling a pivot in the country&#x27;s energy strategy. The move could affect climate goals, energy security, and the pace of nuclear innovation in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士议会投票解除对新核电厂的禁令，标志着能源战略的转折。此举可能影响碳目标、能源安全以及欧洲在核能创新上的推进速度。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 794</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48519092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 596</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dailycal.org/news/campus/academics/failing-grades-soar-as-professors-see-greater-ai-usage-dwindling-math-skills-in-uc-berkeley/article_16fad0bf-02cb-4b8c-8d88-888ffd9f8608.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Failing grades soar with AI usage, dwindling math skills in Berkeley CS classes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 使用激增导致伯克利计算机系课程不及格率攀升，数学能力下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 791</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 753</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley CS instructors report a spike in failing grades as AI usage increases in coursework, accompanied by a perceived decline in math skills among students. The trend underscores a broader tension between AI-assisted learning and mastery of fundamentals, suggesting the need for new assessment methods and curriculum adjustments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 在课程中的使用增加，伯克利计算机科学课程的不及格率攀升，数学基础能力也在下降。这一现象凸显了 AI 辅助学习与基础知识掌握之间的张力，促使课程设计和评估方式需要改革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bloximages.chicago2.vip.townnews.com/dailycal.org/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/c/40/c40f11cf-dfb0-4973-a534-c64044165f16/6a206ddeec122.image.jpg?crop=1793%2C941%2C0%2C106&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' data-full='https://bloximages.chicago2.vip.townnews.com/dailycal.org/content/tncms/assets/v3/editorial/c/40/c40f11cf-dfb0-4973-a534-c64044165f16/6a206ddeec122.image.jpg?crop=1793%2C941%2C0%2C106&amp;resize=1200%2C630&amp;order=crop%2Cresize' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48440792' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://igerman.cc/blog/dopamine-fracking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dopamine Fracking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多巴胺开采</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48440792' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 409</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how apps weaponize dopamine triggers to lock in user attention, i.e., dopamine fracking. It discusses engineering tricks used to maximize engagement and the long-term costs to well-being. It suggests alternative design practices that respect users’ time and autonomy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批判性地分析应用如何利用多巴胺反馈机制来锁定用户注意力，即所谓的‘多巴胺开采’。文中揭示了被用来最大化黏性的一些工程手段及其对长期健康的成本，并提出更具道德性的产品设计思路，强调尊重用户时间与自主权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' data-full='https://igerman.cc/processed_images/Synthetic_Krabby_Patty.313451d7779d4811.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48673671' data-tags='Show HN,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hackernewstrends.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I made Google Trends for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of comments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 18 年评论做成 Hacker News 的 Google 趋势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 788</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hobbyist built a Google Trends-style tool for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of HN comments. It lets you track topic popularity, spikes around events, and compare keywords over time. Demonstrates how public datasets can be mined for long-term trend analysis without official APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位热爱者通过对 18 年 Hacker News 评论进行索引，打造了一个类似 Google Trends 的趋势分析工具。它可以追踪话题热度、事件引发的峰值，并对比不同关键词在时间轴上的变化，展示了在没有官方 API 的情况下对公开数据进行长期趋势分析的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hackernewstrends.com/opengraph-image?72011d0134607af3' data-full='https://hackernewstrends.com/opengraph-image?72011d0134607af3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48477135' data-tags='Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m Eric Ries, author of &quot;The Lean Startup&quot; and new book &quot;Incorruptible&quot; – AMA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是《精益创业》作者 Eric Ries，新书《Incorruptible》— AMA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 781</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48477135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 558</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Eric Ries discusses his new book Incorruptible and takes questions on entrepreneurship, governance, and sustainable product practices. He shares pragmatic perspectives drawn from Lean Startup principles applied to real-world business and technology challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Eric Ries 在 AMA 中讨论新书《Incorruptible》及创业治理、可持续产品实践等话题，结合 Lean Startup 的原则给出面向现实商业与科技挑战的可操作见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48571526' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/35949-volkswagen-app?page=3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Volkswagen started blocking GrapheneOS users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 775</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48571526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 462</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Volkswagen has started blocking GrapheneOS users from its apps. The move highlights ongoing frictions between privacy-focused mobile OS choices and corporate app ecosystems, raising questions about platform-level access and user rights. It underscores how vendor policies can limit privacy-aware users and the ecosystems around open-source mobile OSes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大众汽车开始屏蔽 GrapheneOS 用户使用其应用的行为凸显了隐私导向的移动系统与厂商应用生态之间的矛盾。此举引发对平台访问和用户权益的讨论，也提醒隐私友好型操作系统的使用者可能面临被排除的风险。厂商政策对开源移动生态的影响再次成为焦点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48696585' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec/blob/main/DSpark_paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DSpark: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DSpark：推测解码加速大语言模型推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 775</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DSpark introduces speculative decoding to speed up LLM inference, presenting a method to trade a bit of accuracy for substantial gains in throughput. The paper includes benchmarks and comparisons against baseline decoding strategies, highlighting practical implications for deploying faster AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DSpark 提出一种推测解码方法以加速大语言模型推理，在保持精度损失可控的前提下提升吞吐量。论文给出基准测试和与常规解码的对比，展示在部署更快的 AI 服务时的实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2cbed0be823a39c0476db61ecfc9cfafe5981ecfd4cd97014635836537d5311/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2cbed0be823a39c0476db61ecfc9cfafe5981ecfd4cd97014635836537d5311/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48664814' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/anthropic-says-alibaba-illicitly-extracted-claude-ai-model-capabilities-2026-06-24/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic says Alibaba illicitly extracted Claude AI model capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic称阿里巴巴非法提取Claude AI模型能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic accuses Alibaba of illicitly extracting Claude&#x27;s capabilities, alleging IP theft and copying of proprietary model features. The claim highlights tensions over access to foundation-model technology and could affect licensing, partnerships, and regulatory scrutiny as Alibaba expands its AI stack. If proven, it could spur litigation and reshape cross-border AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 指控阿里巴巴非法提取 Claude 模型的能力，指控涉及知识产权和对专有特征的盗取。此举凸显基础模型领域对接入方式的激烈竞争和潜在法律风险，可能影响许可、伙伴关系及合规审查。若指控成立，可能引发诉讼并重塑跨境 AI 合作格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48537165' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/2026/06/15/curl-summer-of-bliss/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Curl will not accept vulnerability reports during July 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Curl 将在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48537165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The curl project announces a July 2026 hiatus from vulnerability submissions to coordinate disclosures and focus on fixes, with researchers advised to submit reports before or after the window. The move highlights the realities of coordinating security work in a large, open-source project and sets expectations for researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Curl 项目宣布在 2026 年 7 月暂停接受漏洞报告，以便协调披露时间并集中处理修复工作，建议研究人员在此窗口前后提交报告。此举凸显了在大型开源项目中协同安全工作的现实挑战，并为研究人员设定预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curl-summer-of-bliss.jpg' data-full='https://daniel.haxx.se/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/curl-summer-of-bliss.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48387270' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/philosophy/2026/06/no-artificial-intelligence-is-not-conscious/687378/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artificial intelligence is not conscious – Ted Chiang</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能并非具备意识——Ted Chiang</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 765</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48387270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience. The piece further explores ethical and policy implications, urging careful framing of AI capabilities and responsibilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。文章还讨论伦理与政策含义，呼吁对 AI 能力与责任进行谨慎界定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/fdONLsUPMiSaP_UQenVaAXAt2nI=/40x45:1998x1065/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/06/2026_06_01_chiang_Consciousness_Trap_final_horizontal/original.gif' data-full='https://cdn.theatlantic.com/thumbor/fdONLsUPMiSaP_UQenVaAXAt2nI=/40x45:1998x1065/1200x625/media/img/mt/2026/06/2026_06_01_chiang_Consciousness_Trap_final_horizontal/original.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48697560' data-tags='Open Source,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openra.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenRA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenRA 开源实时策略游戏引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 764</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRA remains a vibrant open-source platform that reimagines classic Command &amp; Conquer titles with a modern engine, cross-platform play, and active modding support. It illustrates how community-driven game projects keep retro favorites accessible and extensible long after the original releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRA 作为一个开源平台，持续以现代引擎重新演绎经典指挥与征服系列，支持跨平台对战与活跃的模组生态。该项目证明了社区驱动的游戏项目如何让怀旧游戏在当代保持可访问与可扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openra.net/images/social.jpg?t=2026-04-30-18-11' data-full='https://www.openra.net/images/social.jpg?t=2026-04-30-18-11' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48356625' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malicious npm packages detected across Red Hat Cloud Services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 云服务中检测到恶意 npm 包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48356625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 442</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem. The episode reinforces the importance of dependency hygiene, reproducible builds, and source provenance checks for cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。该事件强调对依赖项的严格管控、可重复构建和来源可信性的重要性，尤其在云环境中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81b6be3beea568b6cd77bff7667f4256cb7a678931e248c9efa036a2f509345b/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81b6be3beea568b6cd77bff7667f4256cb7a678931e248c9efa036a2f509345b/RedHatInsights/javascript-clients/issues/492' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48498573' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/11/fable-is-relentlessly-proactive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable is relentlessly proactive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Fable 的强力主动性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 650</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable demonstrates relentlessly proactive behavior in AI agents, taking initiative to perform tasks without explicit prompts. While this can boost productivity, it also raises governance and safety concerns about autonomy, control, and overreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Fable 展现出在 AI 代理中持续主动的行为，主动承担任务而不需要明确指令。尽管提升了生产力，但也引发关于自主性、控制与超越边界的治理与安全问题的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/jump-to-bug.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/jump-to-bug.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48518684' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jietang/status/2065784751345287314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 Is Out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 已发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48518684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48518684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 475</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 releases bring updates to the graphics math library, with API refinements and performance tweaks for modern rendering pipelines. Useful for developers targeting OpenGL and Vulkan workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 发布，带来 API 精炼和性能优化，帮助开发者在现代渲染管线（OpenGL/Vulkan 等）中更高效地使用图形数学工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cnl5taoq5on9lswk.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/cluster-share-cards/154ee890-ca35-4d87-87f3-3fac9adbffee/1781377947497.png' data-full='https://cnl5taoq5on9lswk.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/cluster-share-cards/154ee890-ca35-4d87-87f3-3fac9adbffee/1781377947497.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48498385' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://web.mit.edu/nelsonr/www/Repenning=Sterman_CMR_su01_.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nobody ever gets credit for fixing problems that never happened (2001) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有人因为修复从未发生的问题而获得名声（2001）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 749</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48498385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2001 paper argues that preventing problems that would have never occurred often goes unrecognized, shaping incentives away from preventive work. It examines how reward structures and management practices reward visible incidents rather than investments in resilience, with broad implications for software quality and risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2001 年的论文指出，阻止原本不太可能发生的问题常常得不到认可，从而改变了对预防性工作的激励机制。文章分析了奖励与管理实践如何偏向对可见事故的处理，而非对提高韧性的投入，对软件质量与风险管理具有广泛影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48383220' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c93x0k194yno' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta workers can opt out of being tracked at work up to 30 min</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 员工可在工作时最多不被追踪30分钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 748</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 724</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta has updated its employee monitoring policy to allow workers to opt out of being tracked for up to 30 minutes at a time. The change underscores the tension between productivity analytics and employee privacy in big tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 更新员工监控政策，允许员工单次最多有30分钟不被追踪。此举凸显了大科技公司在生产力分析与员工隐私之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/947f/live/8311e710-5f35-11f1-b9e6-7bbc2b9cb9c1.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/947f/live/8311e710-5f35-11f1-b9e6-7bbc2b9cb9c1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48630066' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nevergivethemyourface.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Never Give Them Your Face</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永远别让他们得到你的脸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 741</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biometric privacy takes center stage as the piece argues you should not hand over facial data to services. It explains the risks of irreversible exposure and outlines practical steps to minimize biometric leakage and design identity systems with privacy in mind.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章聚焦生物识别隐私，主张避免向服务提供脸部数据，因为一旦暴露难以撤回。文中给出实际步骤以降低面部识别数据的泄露风险，并探讨在身份设计中应考虑的隐私治理要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48384355' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://burntsushi.net/encephalitis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I was recently diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我最近被诊断患上抗 NMDA 受体脑炎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 740</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48384355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account of being diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis, a rare autoimmune condition that can mimic psychiatric illness. The post emphasizes the difficulty of correct diagnosis and the importance of awareness among clinicians and patients. It sheds light on the intersection of neurology and mental health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享罕见的抗 NMDA 受体脑炎诊断经历，这种疾病常因症状类似精神疾病而误诊。文章强调正确诊断的难度，以及提高临床与患者对该病的认知重要性，彰显神经科与精神健康的交叉议题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48538229' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mrmarket.lol/what-the-fuck-happened-to-nerds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happened to nerds?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>书呆子到底怎么了？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48538229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 736</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48538229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 498</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions what happened to the traditional nerd archetype as tech culture shifts and becomes mainstream.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章在质疑随着科技文化的转变，传统书呆子形象到底去了哪里。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mrmarket.lol/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://mrmarket.lol/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707719' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/double-threat-to-private-communications-undemocratic-chat-control-backroom-deals-and-imminent-concessions-spark-relaunch-of-fightchatcontrol-eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU to legislate about Chat Control behind closed doors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟在幕后推动聊天监控立法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 428</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports indicate the EU plans to legislate on Chat Control behind closed doors, raising concerns about transparency and civil liberties. The move signals a push to regulate private communications and could set norms that influence global tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，欧盟正在幕后推动“聊天控制”相关立法，引发对透明度和公民自由的担忧。此举表明欧洲正推动对私人通信的规制，可能对全球科技政策产生重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.macrumors.com/2026/06/08/apple-reveals-new-ai-architecture/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple reveals new AI architecture built around Google Gemini models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果揭示以 Google Gemini 模型为核心的新 AI 架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 555</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils an AI architecture centered on Gemini models, signaling closer integration with Google’s AI stack. The move could influence on-device inference, privacy controls, and cross-platform tooling, shaping the next wave of iOS AI features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果公布以 Gemini 模型为核心的新 AI 架构，预示将更紧密地整合 Google 的 AI 技术栈。此举可能影响设备端推理、隐私控制以及跨平台工具链，并可能塑造未来的 iOS AI 功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.macrumors.com/t/dUIgpIiBn2wAKhWSl0HDOLdc2LU=/1600x/article-new/2026/06/apple-intelligence-architecture.jpg' data-full='https://images.macrumors.com/t/dUIgpIiBn2wAKhWSl0HDOLdc2LU=/1600x/article-new/2026/06/apple-intelligence-architecture.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how Meta handles whistleblowers, including legal tactics and public messaging that factions describe as aggressive suppression. The piece surveys the implications for corporate accountability, transparency, and whistleblower protections.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48553550' data-tags='Hardware,Energy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciechanow.ski/mechanical-watch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanical Watch (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机械表（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 715</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48553550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mechanical Watch (2022) breaks down the fundamentals: a mainspring stores energy, a gear train transmits it, and the escapement and balance wheel regulate time. It also discusses sources of error—friction, temperature, and wear—and why accuracy remains a balancing act between durability and precision.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《机械手表（2022）》分解原理：发条储存能量，通过齿轮传动，靠擒纵机构和摆轮来调控走时。文中还讨论了摩擦、温度和磨损等误差来源，以及在耐用性与精准度之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ciechanow.ski/images/og/watch.png' data-full='https://ciechanow.ski/images/og/watch.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://point.free/blog/gemma-4-on-a-2016-xeon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 10 year old Xeon is all you need</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一颗十年前的 Xeon 就足够用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48353348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 711</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48353348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 281</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decade-old Xeon can still power modern workloads if software is tuned for efficiency. The post demonstrates competitive results on a 2016-era server CPU, encouraging a rethink of hardware budgets for modest-scale deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过优化的软件仍能让十年前的 Xeon 功能不错地应对现代工作负载。该案例在 2016 年代的服务器 CPU 上展现出具有竞争力的性能，促使小型部署重新考量硬件预算。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://point.free/processed_images/banner.ebd2671bea295a74.jpg' data-full='https://point.free/processed_images/banner.ebd2671bea295a74.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dronexl.co/2026/06/09/pokemon-go-scans-niantic-vantor-military-drone-navigation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pokémon Go Scans Trained the Navigation Tech for Military Drones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宝可梦GO 扫描数据训练军事无人机导航技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48487029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dataset trained from Pokémon Go scans was used to train navigation tech for military drones, illustrating how crowdsourced, consumer data can power defense-grade AI. The case underscores dual-use AI and raises questions about privacy and consent as civilian data helps weaponized navigation systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宝可梦 GO 的扫描数据被用于训练军事无人机的导航技术，展示了众包数据在防务级 AI 中的双重用途。此事凸显隐私与数据许可的挑战，以及民用数据被用于武器化导航系统的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dronexl.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Pokemon-Go-Scans-Quietly-Trained-the-Navigation-Tech-Now-Headed-Into-Military-Drones.jpg' data-full='https://dronexl.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Pokemon-Go-Scans-Quietly-Trained-the-Navigation-Tech-Now-Headed-Into-Military-Drones.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48649011' data-tags='Career,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/JPoehnelt/status/2069482265953087602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fired by Google for creating the Google workspace CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因为创建 Google Workspace CLI 而被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48649011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48649011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former Google employee reports being fired for building the Google Workspace CLI, raising questions about internal policies toward external tooling and open-source contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称一名前 Google 员工因开发 Google Workspace CLI 而被解雇，引发关于内部政策对外部工具和开源贡献态度的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48427643' data-tags='AI,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://this.weekinsecurity.com/meta-confirms-thousands-of-instagram-accounts-were-hacked-by-abusing-its-ai-chatbot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta confirms 1000s of Instagram accounts were hacked by abusing its AI chatbot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 确认数千 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering. The incident underscores security risks in consumer AI assistants and the need for safer safeguards and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。事件凸显消费级 AI 助手在安全性方面的风险，以及对更安全防护与监控的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/meta-ai-chatbot-twis-story-2.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ed/a2/eda2c6f7-faef-48b4-9ed4-86a4fa4dca68/content/images/size/w1200/2026/06/meta-ai-chatbot-twis-story-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48406174' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: What was your &quot;oh shit&quot; moment with GenAI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：你在 GenAI 使用中的‘哦糟了’时刻是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48406174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1084</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community prompt asking for the worst or funniest &#x27;oh shit&#x27; moments with GenAI, highlighting surprising failures, hallucinations, and ethical concerns. It gathers practical anecdotes for risk awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区发起 Ask HN，征集关于在生成式 AI 使用过程中的“哦糟了”时刻，揭示模型幻觉、局限及伦理风险等真实案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48364055' data-tags='Finance,Business,AI,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/06/01/can-the-stockmarket-swallow-anthropic-spacex-and-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can the stockmarket swallow Anthropic, SpaceX and OpenAI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>股市能否容纳Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI. It highlights structural constraints like liquidity, dilution, and investor risk appetite that could slow up-rounds for highly valued private firms. The takeaway is that public markets may cap how quickly such companies can scale via public offerings, shaping their financing strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。它强调流动性、股本稀释和投资者风险偏好等结构性约束，可能放缓高估值私有公司的轮融资。核心观点是公开市场可能限制这些公司通过上市快速扩张的能力，从而影响它们的融资策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48584207' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/06/15/microsofts-new-outlook-takes-10-seconds-to-do-what-outlook-classic-does-instantly-on-windows/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft new Outlook takes 10 seconds to do what Outlook Classic does instantly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软新版 Outlook 比经典版慢十秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48584207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 485</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The new Outlook design takes about ten seconds to complete tasks that the classic app did instantly, highlighting a productivity hit from modernization. This reflects a broader tension between feature-rich updates and user experience in enterprise software, fueling calls for an option to revert to a familiar workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新版 Outlook 在完成一些经典版能瞬间完成的任务时需约十秒，暴露出现代化改造带来的生产力损失。这反映了企业软件在功能增强与用户体验之间的博弈，也推动了对保留熟悉工作流的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Clicking-Outlook-notifications-will-take-longer-to-load-the-email-than-if-you-open-the-app-and-find-the-mail-directly.jpg' data-full='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Clicking-Outlook-notifications-will-take-longer-to-load-the-email-than-if-you-open-the-app-and-find-the-mail-directly.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48730729' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waag.org/en/article/european-digital-id-wallets-are-gift-google-and-apple/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European digital ID wallets rely on safety services of Google and Apple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲数字身份证钱包依赖谷歌和苹果的安全服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems. While this brings strong security and interoperability, it raises questions about data sovereignty, governance, and vendor dependence as Europe pushes self-sovereign identity models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。与此同时，这种依赖也引发数据主权、治理和对厂商依赖的担忧，或影响未来的自我主权身份框架的推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cms.waag.org/sites/waag/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026-06/scherm-afbeelding_2026-06-23_om_10.31.35.png' data-full='https://cms.waag.org/sites/waag/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026-06/scherm-afbeelding_2026-06-23_om_10.31.35.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swipe.futo.tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FUTO Swipe – A new swipe typing model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FUTO Swipe 轻扫输入模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FUTO Swipe introduces a new swipe-typing model designed to improve accuracy and speed on mobile keyboards. The model promises smarter prediction and resilience across layouts, with a public demo at the project site.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FUTO Swipe 推出全新的滑动输入模型，旨在提升移动端键盘的准确性与输入速度。该模型声称在不同布局下都具备更智能的预测能力，公开演示在项目站点可见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://swipe.futo.tech/hero-2.webp' data-full='https://swipe.futo.tech/hero-2.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
