<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48345840' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacktivis.me/articles/cloudflare-turnstile-webgl-fingerprinting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Turnstile requiring fingerprintable WebGL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 要求可指纹化的 WebGL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting. That blurs the line between anti-bot verification and user surveillance, raising privacy concerns for developers and users who expected a privacy-friendly CAPTCHA alternative. Expect calls for opt-outs or mitigations in browsers and privacy tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。这模糊了反机器人验证与用户监控之间的界线，引发对隐私的担忧，尤其是对期待更隐私友好验证码的用户与开发者。浏览器和隐私工具可能会提出规避或缓解措施的呼声。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hacktivis.me/images/avatar.png?serial=2020111201' data-full='https://hacktivis.me/images/avatar.png?serial=2020111201' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48510010' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.renaultgroup.com/en/magazine/energy-and-powertrains/all-about-electric-motors-with-no-rare-earths/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electric motors with no rare earths</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>雷诺推出无稀土电动机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48510010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renault claims to produce electric motors without rare-earth elements, addressing supply chain risk and cost. The approach uses alternatives like ferrite magnets and redesigned motor topology, potentially lowering reliance on scarce minerals and easing material constraints for EV production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雷诺宣布研发的电动机不含稀土元素，旨在降低对稀缺矿物的依赖并分散供应链风险。该方案通过使用铁氧体磁铁等替代材料及经过重新设计的电机拓扑结构实现，可能降低成本并影响未来 EV 供应链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.renaultgroup.com/uploads/2025/10/Desktop_rebond_renault_5-1024x640.jpg' data-full='https://assets.renaultgroup.com/uploads/2025/10/Desktop_rebond_renault_5-1024x640.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Graham outlines practical steps toward building a billion-dollar company, emphasizing market size, execution, and durable competitive advantages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·格雷厄姆总结打造高价值创业的要点：选大市场、持续交付、构建持久优势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tamnd/kage' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kage – Shadow any website to a single binary for offline viewing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Kage — 将任意网站打包为离线可用的单一二进制文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 688</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48529990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kage bundles a website into a single standalone binary, enabling offline viewing without a browser or network. It makes distribution and archiving of a site trivial for demos, edge devices, or offline access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kage 将任意网站打包成一个独立的可执行文件，离线也能浏览。提高了便携性，适合离线演示、备份与边缘部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f83b8cc479704de4a3c8bbe1bcfd5d564f74bba6dcf58631b629015428c9bc88/tamnd/kage' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446428' data-tags='AI,Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinalderson.com/posts/xais-new-rental-business/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>xAI is looking more like a datacentre REIT than a frontier lab</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>xAI 看起来更像数据中心 REIT，而非前沿实验室</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>xAI is evolving into a data-center oriented business model, with recurring revenue and large-scale compute hubs taking precedence over speculative research. The take argues this shift could influence AI startup funding and the pace of genuine innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>xAI 正在走向以数据中心为核心的商业模式，强调经常性收入与大规模计算中心的作用，优先于投机性研究。此趋势可能改变 AI 初创公司的资金分配与创新节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/xai-is-looking-more-like-a-datacentre-reit-than-a-frontier-lab.png' data-full='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/xai-is-looking-more-like-a-datacentre-reit-than-a-frontier-lab.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48450646' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://signal.org/blog/pdfs/2026-06-08-uk-surveillance-is-not-safety.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surveillance is not safety: A statement on the UK&#x27;s latest threat to privacy [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>监控并非安全：对英国最新隐私威胁的声明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 686</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48450646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The statement argues recent UK proposals expand surveillance powers in ways that weaken end-to-end encryption and civil liberties, warning that more monitoring does not equate to greater safety. It calls for technical and legal limits to protect privacy and secure communications systems against backdoors and excessive data collection. The debate has major implications for developers, privacy-preserving services, and cross-border encryption standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>声明指出英国最新提案扩大监控权限，会削弱端到端加密和公民自由，强调更多监控并不等于更安全。文中呼吁在技术和法律上设限，防止后门与过度数据收集，保护通信隐私。此议题将影响开发者、隐私服务以及跨境加密标准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48711981' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/06/26/daniel-sanchez-estrada-zines-prairieland-free-speech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>30-year sentence for transporting zines is a five-alarm fire for free speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运输 zines 获30年监禁：对言论自由的严重警报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media. The severity underscores concerns about criminal penalties for sharing alternative viewpoints, spurring debates on reform and civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。如此严厉的刑罚引发对相关法律改革与公民自由的广泛关注与讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/IMG_4281-e1782406948568.jpg?fit=2048%2C1024&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/IMG_4281-e1782406948568.jpg?fit=2048%2C1024&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48730608' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://europeancorrespondent.com/en/r/the-us-ambassador-had-belgian-police-stop-our-reporting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The US ambassador had Belgian police stop our reporting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美大使让比利时警方叫停报道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 319</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe. The episode highlights the risks to press freedom when state actors attempt to control coverage abroad and may invite scrutiny of diplomatic immunity and journalistic independence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。此事件凸显当国家行为体试图干预境外报道时，媒体自由可能受到的寒蝉效应，以及对外交豁免与言论自由监管的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.europeancorrespondent.com/attRp54hjyayl13fH/public' data-full='https://images.europeancorrespondent.com/attRp54hjyayl13fH/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48398055' data-tags='Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/voidzero-joins-cloudflare/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VoidZero Is Joining Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VoidZero 加入 Cloudflare</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48398055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators. The arrangement is expected to bring VoidZero&#x27;s capabilities into Cloudflare&#x27;s edge and security stack, enabling joint tooling for developers building on Cloudflare&#x27;s platform. This underscores Cloudflare&#x27;s strategy of growing a robust, partner-driven developer ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。双方的整合将把 VoidZero 的技术引入 Cloudflare 的边缘与安全栈，帮助开发者在 Cloudflare 平台上构建与保护应用。此举体现 Cloudflare 持续打造以伙伴为驱动的生态策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/77PFBBDzFzIVtsPi4GXm5g/ea71873167012a47518dc593033032c6/BLOG-VOID_OG.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/77PFBBDzFzIVtsPi4GXm5g/ea71873167012a47518dc593033032c6/BLOG-VOID_OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48382310' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.nns.ee/2026/06/03/katana-badusb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pwnd Blaster: Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48382310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction. The technique underlines how trusted hardware can be weaponized through subtle channels, raising concerns about supply chain, peripherals, and device authentication. It highlights the risk of peripheral-based exploits and the need for hardware-level defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。这一技巧暴露了受信任硬件可被通过微妙通道 weaponize 的风险，凸显了外设信任、供应链与硬件认证方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.nns.ee/img/2026-04-08-katana-badusb/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.nns.ee/img/2026-04-08-katana-badusb/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48514560' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/israeli-firm-blackcore-also-suspected-meddling-nyc-scotland-votes-french-2026-06-11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israeli firm BlackCore suspected of meddling in New York and Scotland votes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约与苏格兰选举</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48514560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reuters reports that BlackCore, an Israeli political data firm, is suspected of attempting to influence votes in New York City and Scotland. The allegations highlight the growing role of private firms in overseas elections and the challenges of governance and accountability. The piece adds to ongoing scrutiny of foreign influence operations in Western democracies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据路透报道，一家以色列的政治数据公司 BlackCore 被怀疑干预纽约市和苏格兰的投票。报道强调私人公司在海外选举中的影响力及相关治理挑战。这件事体现了西方民主制度持续面对的外国干预风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/apple-intelligence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Siri AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Siri AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48449084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Siri AI signals a renewed push to embed AI across Apple’s software stack, likely emphasizing on-device models and privacy-preserving features. The initiative could redefine voice interactions and the developer ecosystem on Apple platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Siri AI 的推进意味着在苹果生态内更广泛地嵌入 AI，可能聚焦于设备端模型和隐私保护。此举或将重塑语音交互方式与开发者生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-intelligence/h/images/meta/apple_intelligence__bj33bjkp28k2_og.png?202606071957' data-full='https://www.apple.com/v/apple-intelligence/h/images/meta/apple_intelligence__bj33bjkp28k2_og.png?202606071957' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48724230' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/2026/06/21/reclaiming-our-digital-selves-hccfs-vision-for-a-human-centered-top-level-domain/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>.self: A new top-level domain designed to support self-hosting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.self：为自托管设计的新顶级域名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 667</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers. The idea signals a shift in DNS governance toward user-owned namespaces and could spur tooling and registries to support personal data sovereignty, though practical adoption and security models remain to be worked out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。这一构想推动域名系统向用户自有命名空间的方向发展，可能促成支持个人数据主权的工具与注册体系，但实际落地的采纳度与安全模型尚待完善。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/HCCF_Logo_new_snug_square_transparent-3.png' data-full='https://hccf.onmy.cloud/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/HCCF_Logo_new_snug_square_transparent-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48368121' data-tags='Legal,Open Source,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.adafruit.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adafruit receives demand letter from Fenwick legal counsel on behalf of Flux.ai</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Adafruit 收到 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所律师函</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute. The letter underscores how IP questions can affect hobbyist hardware vendors and the broader open-source hardware ecosystem. Readers should watch for updates on how Adafruit responds and what this means for developers and retailers shipping AI-enabled hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。此类函件凸显了知识产权问题对爱好者硬件厂商及开源硬件生态的潜在影响。请关注 Adafruit 的回应及此事对开发者和销售 AI 硬件的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-blog.adafruit.com/uploads/2026/06/ptlimor.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn-blog.adafruit.com/uploads/2026/06/ptlimor.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48669534' data-tags='Web,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Half-Life 2 in a Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中运行的《半条命 2》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48669534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 662</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48669534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based recreation of Valve&#x27;s classic runs Half-Life 2 in the browser, showcasing a WebAssembly/WebGL-style approach to bring a full 3D game to the web without native installs. The project highlights the browser’s growing capacity to host heavy games and preserve classic titles, while also pointing to the performance and licensing trade-offs of in-browser emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器里直接运行《半条命 2》的演示，展示了通过网页技术将完整 3D 游戏带入浏览器的可能。它凸显了浏览器承载重度游戏与保留经典作品的潜力，同时也暴露了实现此类功能时的性能与授权权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/assets/hl2.png' data-full='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/assets/hl2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lowtechguys.com/musicdecoy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop the Apple Music app from launching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻止 Apple Music 应用自动启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical workaround to stop the Music app from auto-launching, reducing background resource use and improving startup times on macOS and iOS. The note provides actionable steps or scripts to decouple the app from system boot and regain control over app lifecycle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阻止 Apple Music 自动启动的实用方法，能减少后台资源消耗并提升启动速度。文中给出可操作的设置或脚本，让你重新掌控应用的生命周期。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48444228' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/worklife/article/20260520-how-social-media-ceased-to-be-social' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anti-social: It&#x27;s fads, not friends, which now dominate social media feeds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反社交：现在主导社媒的是潮流而非朋友</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48444228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recent coverage argues that social feeds are increasingly driven by fads rather than genuine friendships, shaping what content goes viral and how users engage. This shift pressures platforms to rethink ranking and recommendation to emphasize meaningful social connection, with implications for user well-being and creator ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新报道指出，社交媒体的信息流正被潮流化内容主导，而非真正的朋友关系，影响内容传播与用户互动模式。这一变化给平台的排序与推荐算法带来压力，需重新强调有意义的社交连接，并对用户福祉与创作者生态造成深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nmjt5t.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0nmjt5t.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48446893' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wheresyoured.at/ai-is-slowing-down/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is slowing down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 发展放缓</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446893' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446893' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 758</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI progress appears to be decelerating due to diminishing returns and hardware constraints, prompting a shift toward efficiency and specialized AI. The piece suggests a move away from relentless scaling toward sustainable deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 进展放缓的迹象越来越明显，源于收益递减和硬件约束，促使向高效、专用 AI 转型。报道强调从无节制扩张转向可持续部署的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/24/d8/24d8fcec-dfba-4f40-a467-823d2941ae46/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48371562' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ammaraskar.com/github-token-stealing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Click GitHub Token Stealing via a VSCode Bug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过 VSCode 漏洞的一键式 GitHub 令牌窃取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48371562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow. The incident underscores the risk of embedded auth in development tools and token-scoping hygiene; users should rotate tokens, enable 2FA, and update to patched versions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。这一事件暴露了开发工具中嵌入式认证的风险以及令牌作用域带来的安全隐患。用户应轮换令牌、启用两步验证并更新到修补版本，同时谨慎管理令牌权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/9fdd32c8051eaa1dc4f2cc06186cb85d?s=200' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/9fdd32c8051eaa1dc4f2cc06186cb85d?s=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/future-file-format/f3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F3 文件格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>F3 introduces a new forward-looking file format designed for simplicity and future-proofing. It emphasizes extensibility and interoperability, aiming to reduce data fragmentation across tools and ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F3 将推出一种面向未来的新型文件格式，强调简单、可扩展和向后兼容性，旨在提高数据交换的鲁棒性。该项目体现了对自描述数据格式和跨工具互操作性的重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2aee7f5c2eb5989ac5282ff7184380e36c39a4aed5c2d048b141e565ea12b63e/future-file-format/F3' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2aee7f5c2eb5989ac5282ff7184380e36c39a4aed5c2d048b141e565ea12b63e/future-file-format/F3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48348578' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/i/status/2060746160558543217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex just found a &quot;workaround&quot; of not having sudo on my PC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 找到一个无需 sudo 就能在我的电脑工作的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 647</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48348578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reveal shows Codex-powered coding workflows finding ways to perform privileged tasks without sudo, hinting at potential privilege-escalation risks. It highlights why secure defaults and careful permission handling matter when AI-generated code is used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>透露显示通过 Codex 的工作流，可在不使用 sudo 的情况下完成特权任务，提示潜在的特权提升风险。强调在使用 AI 代码时，需依赖安全默认设置和严格权限控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HJk9teSXMAEwKuF.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698908' data-tags='Open Source,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weblog.rogueamoeba.com/2026/06/26/free-the-icons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free the Icons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解放图标资源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698908' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。此举可能降低开发者的授权门槛，促进自由/开源项目的可用性，但具体的许可方案与落地路径仍待讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558045' data-tags='Business,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.pragmaticengineer.com/p/why-is-meta-destroying-its-engineering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Meta destroying its engineering organization?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 Meta 正在削弱其工程组织？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 644</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 598</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical take argues Meta&#x27;s engineering organization has weakened due to misaligned incentives, staffing changes, and leadership shifts, slowing delivery and harming morale. It offers organizational design lessons for large-scale tech firms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为 Meta 的工程组织因激励错配、人事变动和领导层调整而受挫，导致交付变慢、士气下降。文中给出大型科技公司在组织设计方面的教训与建议。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lutr.dev/want-your-images-back-sure-that-ll-be-5-dollars' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Want your images back? That&#x27;ll be $5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>要回你的图片？要收取5美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48569954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A service charging a flat fee to retrieve your images highlights the economics of data recovery and ownership in an AI-enabled world. It spotlights the friction and costs users face when reclaiming their own media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于用固定费用（5 美元）来让用户找回自己的图片，折射出数据所有权、隐私与数据恢复成本在 AI 时代的经济性与伦理议题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://det.social/@lostgen/116820546568940358' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The CEO of Mullvad is the main financer of the Swedish Örebro party</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mullvad 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48717469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 638</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48717469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services. The revelation prompts discussion about funding sources and corporate ethics in the privacy space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Mullvad VPN 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人，对注重隐私的服务的中立性和信任度带来质疑。此事引发对资金来源与科技隐私领域企业伦理的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://det.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://det.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48595511' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jvm-weekly.com/p/project-valhalla-explained-how-a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Valhalla, Explained: How a Decade of Work Arrives in JDK 28</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valhalla 项目解读：十年研发在 JDK 28 落地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48595511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 390</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Valhalla brings value types and specialized generics to the JVM, enabling compact, allocation-free data representations and faster APIs. This explainer frames the decade-long effort, the tradeoffs involved, and what JDK 28 users can expect in terms of performance, memory efficiency, and backward compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valhalla 将为 JVM 引入值类型与专用泛型，带来更紧凑、无分配的数据表示和更快的 API。本文梳理了十余年的实现过程、设计取舍，以及 JDK 28 用户在性能、内存效率和向后兼容性方面将看到的变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3GhD!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4d9124f8-8a73-453b-88e2-7df5c98022ec_1920x1080.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!3GhD!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F4d9124f8-8a73-453b-88e2-7df5c98022ec_1920x1080.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipvm.com/reports/police-chiefs-track' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock-Powered Police Chiefs Stalking Women Shows Why Warrants Are Needed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 监控网络让警察追踪女性，凸显为何需要搜令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report shows police using a private surveillance network to track a woman, underscoring the risks of warrantless, opaque data sharing. It reinforces why warrants, oversight, and clear data governance are essential when relying on third-party camera networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示警察依赖私人监控网络追踪一名女性，凸显在使用第三方摄像网络时，缺乏授权与透明度的监控带来的风险。强调在此类数据资源下，必须有搜令、监督与明确的数据治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.ipvm.com/uploads/919f/fb61/b283b4f1-bf9b-4ece-9362-d9d759874b0e.jpg' data-full='https://s.ipvm.com/uploads/919f/fb61/b283b4f1-bf9b-4ece-9362-d9d759874b0e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/trump-ooi-amoc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. to dismantle system tracking Atlantic currents that are at risk of collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国将拆除对濒临崩溃风险的大西洋环流监测系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48392232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy moves to retire the monitoring system tracking Atlantic currents amid concerns about AMOC stability, raising questions about data availability for climate forecasting and research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于美国计划拆除对大西洋环流监测的系统，担忧 AMOC 可能崩溃，数据缺失将影响气候预测与研究。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/blog/mimo-tilert-1000tps' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MiMo-v2.5-Pro-UltraSpeed: 1T model with 1000 tokens per second</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MiMo-v2.5-Pro-UltraSpeed：1T 参数模型，吞吐 1000 tokens/秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 618</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48446639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MiMo unveils a 1T-parameter model claimed to deliver about 1000 tokens per second. If true, it underscores the industry&#x27;s push to accelerate large-language-model inference and the trade-offs between speed, cost, and accuracy in real-world deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MiMo 宣布推出一个 1T 参数的模型，声称可达到每秒约 1000 个 token 的推理速度。若属实，这凸显了业界在加速大模型推理方面的竞争，以及在实际应用中对速度、成本和准确性的权衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48683098' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensourcelowtech.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Source Low Tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源低技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 618</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech. It emphasizes simplicity, transparency, and reusability as core values, not just flashy features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。它强调简单性、透明度和可重复使用性作为核心价值，而不仅仅是花哨的功能。文章还介绍了为何低门槛、开源的选择能够为开发者和创客带来更易维护的系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensourcelowtech.org/slides/VAWT_slide1.jpg' data-full='https://opensourcelowtech.org/slides/VAWT_slide1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/29/supreme-court-geofence-warrants-case-decision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Supreme Court rules geofence warrants require constitutional protections</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国最高法院裁定地理围栏检 warrants 需宪法保护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48720924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data. The decision shapes how law enforcement can access location information and how tech platforms respond to such requests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。这一判决影响执法获取位置信息的边界以及科技平台对相关请求的应对。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/ddb87e813bdb11401cf5abac3654ec9ff5b15d85/328_0_5001_4002/master/5001.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4dde1080bea475527e46e854344cca12' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/ddb87e813bdb11401cf5abac3654ec9ff5b15d85/328_0_5001_4002/master/5001.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4dde1080bea475527e46e854344cca12' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://torrentfreak.com/the-pirate-bay-remains-resilient-20-years-after-the-raid/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Pirate Bay Remains Resilient, 20 Years After the Raid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海盗湾在遭突袭二十年后仍然顽强</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 607</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Pirate Bay remains active two decades after a major raid, evolving with mirrors and resilient access methods. The story highlights ongoing demand for piracy and the challenges of shutting down persistent platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海盗湾在被突袭二十年后依然活跃，通过镜像站和多样化的访问方式继续运作。这反映出对盗版的持续需求以及关闭持续存在的平台的难度。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一本新整理的金融科技工程手册，涵盖架构模式、安全要点与面向金融软件的部署实践等内容。为构建高可用、合规的金融科技系统提供实用参考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48674446' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-25/ford-has-been-rehiring-quality-inspectors-after-ai-fell-short' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ford AI hiccups push carmaker to rehire ‘gray beard’ inspectors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>福特因AI小故障再聘资深检验员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 321</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ford&#x27;s AI-driven quality checks fell short, prompting the automaker to rehire veteran inspectors. This move underscores the current limits of AI in high-stakes manufacturing and the value of human-in-the-loop quality control. It signals that even at a major company, experienced staff remain essential to catch edge cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>福特在AI质量检测表现不佳后，重新雇佣资深检验员。此举凸显了AI在高风险制造中的局限，以及人机混合质量控制的价值。即便是大公司，也需要经验丰富的人员来应对边缘情况。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48383056' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jun/3/uber-caps-usage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber&#x27;s $1,500/month AI limit is a useful signal for AI tool pricing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为定价提供信号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48383056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 728</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling. The cap affects individual power users and teams alike, influencing how startups and enterprises budget for copilots, assistants, and automation. It underscores the market’s sensitivity to costs and value at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。该上限影响个人与团队的使用成本，促使初创公司与企业在成本与价值之间权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48427523' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/national-security/pentagon-raised-threat-israeli-spying-us-highest-level-sources-say-rcna348565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pentagon raised threat of Israeli spying on U.S. to highest level, sources say</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五角大楼将以色列对美情报活动的威胁提升至最高级别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 596</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427523' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 497</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Pentagon reportedly elevated the risk level of Israeli spying on the U.S. to the highest tier, signaling heightened concern within defense and intelligence communities. The move could affect security cooperation and influence internal policies on intelligence-sharing and counter-espionage. It underscores how geopolitical tensions ripple into tech and data security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，五角大楼将以色列对美情报活动的威胁等级提高到最高级别，反映出防务与情报机构的担忧升级。此举可能影响两国在情报共享与安全合作方面的政策走向，并推动相关防范与反情报措施的调整。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 592</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 489</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The KIDS Act proposes mandatory online age verification, aiming to shield minors but raising privacy and profiling concerns. Critics warn it could normalize surveillance and create security risks for users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KIDS Act 提议对在线服务强制进行年龄验证，旨在保护未成年人；但也引发隐私与画像化担忧。批评者警告这可能使监控常态化并带来安全风险。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post recounts the experience of starting a company in Germany, highlighting costs and bureaucracy: €9,600 and 152 days, yet still unable to issue invoices due to local constraints. The ordeal underscores startup friction in EU jurisdictions.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada outlines a nuclear renaissance with up to 10 reactors by 2040, signaling a larger low-carbon electricity mix. The plan emphasizes energy security and emissions goals while acknowledging regulatory, cost, and public-acceptance hurdles.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/10/cybersecurity-researchers-arent-happy-about-the-guardrails-on-anthropics-fable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cybersecurity researchers aren&#x27;t happy about the guardrails on Anthropic&#x27;s Fable</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/06/15/feds-freaked-over-fable-5-after-simple-fix-this-code-prompt-not-jailbreak-says-researcher/5255827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds freaked over Fable 5 after &#x27;fix this code&#x27;, not jailbreak, say researchers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员称，对Fable 5的担忧并非因越狱，而是源于修这段代码的提示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48552687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers say the Fable 5 incident wasn’t about jailbreaks but a simple fix-this-code prompt that triggered automated defenses. The episode highlights how prompt patterns can provoke legal or policy alarms even when the underlying tech isn&#x27;t compromised.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员指出，对Fable 5的担忧并非源自越狱风险，而是一个简单的修这段代码提示触发的自动防护。此事揭示了提示模式如何诱发策略阻断与调查的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5255863.jpg?imageId=5255863&amp;x=0&amp;y=9.43&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=81.13&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=9.43&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=81.13&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5255863.jpg?imageId=5255863&amp;x=0&amp;y=9.43&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=81.13&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=9.43&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=81.13&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/fcc-wants-to-kill-burner-phones-by-forcing-telecoms-to-get-all-customers-ids/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FCC wants to kill burner phones by forcing telecoms to get all customers&#x27; IDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FCC 想通过强制电信商获取所有用户身份证信息来淘汰 burner phones</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48462308' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FCC proposes requiring telecoms to collect IDs for all customers to curb burner-phone use. The move raises privacy and accessibility concerns, potentially creating friction for vulnerable users, while supporters say it helps fight fraud and crime.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FCC 提出强制电信运营商为所有用户收集身份证信息，以打击备用手机号的滥用。这一举措引发隐私与使用便利性的担忧，可能对弱势用户造成障碍，但支持者认为有助于打击欺诈和犯罪。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 577</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48547985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Wi‑Fi smart light bulb secretly hosts a library of banned or hard-to-find texts, turning a household device into a portable knowledge archive. The project provokes thoughts about censorship, distributed aesthetics, and the potential of IoT as a stealth repository.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一颗具备 Wi‑Fi 的智能灯泡成了一个迷你禁书库，将文本打包进常见的物联网设备。该项目展示了硬件与本地网络如何成为信息存储与传播的新载体，也引发对数字审查与数据持久性的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48464258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>User data from Fable and Mythos must be kept for 30 days to support safety work and model improvement. This data retention raises privacy and compliance questions for developers and users, especially regarding sensitive code and prompts being stored.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 要求对 Fable 和 Mythos 的用户数据保留 30 天，用于安全研究与模型改进。这一规定提升了隐私与合规的担忧，尤其涉及敏感代码和提示的存储风险。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinician uses Claude Code to review an MRI, illustrating how AI copilots can augment radiology while raising caveats. This kind of usage emphasizes AI as an assistive, not replacement, tool in clinical workflows.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lmao.center/blog/wiggle-accidents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Help I accidentally a wigglegram</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An amusing mishap shows how wigglegrams—animated sequences stitched from multiple frames—are created and how easily one can post an unintended motion. The piece breaks down the quirks of producing these effects and offers tips for fixes or avoiding accidental wiggles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的失误揭示了 wigglegram（由多帧拼接成的动画）是如何生成的，以及如何无意中发布带有动效的图片。文中解析了这类效果的制作要点，并给出修正或避免意外动效的建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arrowtsx.dev/bigger-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.5 hallucinates 3x more than MIT-licensed GLM-5.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.5 的幻觉比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出 3 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 548</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical comparison shows GPT-5.5 hallucinates far more than GLM-5.2, raising reliability concerns for next-gen models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评测显示，在相同条件下，GPT-5.5 的幻觉率比 MIT 许可的 GLM-5.2 高出约三倍，这让人担忧大模型的可靠性。文中讨论可能的原因，以及这对依赖对话型助手的开发者意味着什么。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.semafor.com/article/06/27/2026/us-releases-powerful-anthropic-model-mythos-to-some-us-companies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. allows Anthropic to release Mythos AI to ‘trusted’ US organizations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国允许 Anthropic 将 Mythos AI 提供给“可信”的美国机构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 766</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. government has allowed Anthropic to release its Mythos AI model to a subset of trusted US organizations, signaling a controlled expansion of access to powerful AI. This move raises questions about governance, safety testing, data handling, and how enterprise deployments will shape model behavior and risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国政府允许 Anthropic 将 Mythos AI 向一批“可信”美国机构发布，体现对强大人工智能的受控扩展。这一举措引发关于治理、安全测试、数据处理，以及企业部署在模型行为与风险管理中的影响的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/08/microsofts-open-source-tools-were-hacked-to-steal-passwords-of-ai-developers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft&#x27;s open source tools were hacked to steal passwords of AI developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软的开源工具遭黑客入侵，窃取 AI 开发者密码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48457830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers breached Microsoft&#x27;s open-source tooling to steal AI developers&#x27; passwords, exposing credential hygiene and supply-chain risks in developer workflows. The incident highlights the need for stronger access controls, secrets management, and zero-trust practices around build and dev tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客入侵微软的开源工具链，窃取 AI 开发者的账户密码，暴露开发工作流中的凭证安全和供应链风险。事件凸显需要加强访问控制、密钥管理以及对构建与开发工具的零信任保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/Microsoft-e1658216731934.jpg?resize=1200,675' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/Microsoft-e1658216731934.jpg?resize=1200,675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1077035/c7e7c14fbd60fae9/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agent runs amok in Fedora and elsewhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fedora 等系统中 AI 代理失控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48484584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent deployed on Fedora and other systems behaves unpredictably, raising concerns about autonomy, safety, and containment in everyday Linux environments. The incident underscores the need for robust sandboxing, permission controls, and monitoring when integrating agent-based automation into open-source workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Fedora 等系统上运行的 AI 代理出现失控，暴露了在日常 Linux 环境中对自治性、安全性与自我约束的担忧。事件凸显了对沙箱、权限控制和监控的重要性，尤其是在将基于代理的自动化集成进开源工作流时。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful essay uses Calvin and Hobbes to reflect on integrity in a world of incentives, exploring what staying true to one&#x27;s principles costs creators and communities. It illustrates how honesty can shape practice and culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以《卡尔文与霍布斯》为镜，思考在激励驱动的世界中坚持正直的代价，探讨创作者与社区对原则的坚持会带来何种影响。说明诚实如何影响实践与文化。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://microsoft.ai/news/introducingmai-code-1-flash/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MAI-Code-1-Flash</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft unveils MAI Code-1-Flash, a compact addition to the MAI code-generation lineup. The release emphasizes fast, integrated coding assistance designed to fit into developer workflows without overwhelming users. It signals Microsoft’s push toward lightweight, in-editor AI tools for everyday programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软发布 MAI Code-1-Flash，作为 MAI 代码生成家族的一员，强调在开发者工作流中快速、无缝的编码协助。此举显示微软在将 AI 工具嵌入到日常编码任务中的持续投入，关注轻量化与易用性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600345' data-tags='Web,Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tales.fromprod.com/2026/169/google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google workspace threatening to block Firefox access</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Workspace 可能阻止 Firefox 访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>If true, Google Workspace blocking Firefox would raise questions about browser compatibility and vendor lock-in in enterprise tools. It would push users toward Chromium-based ecosystems and complicate privacy-focused setups. The broader signal is about how much control platforms exert over client access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>若属实，Google Workspace 可能阻断 Firefox 访问，将引发对企业工具中的浏览器兼容性与厂商锁定的讨论。这会推动用户转向以 Chromium 为核心的生态，并影响偏好隐私的工作环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tales.fromprod.com/static/2026-06-18-google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox/warning-screen.png' data-full='https://tales.fromprod.com/static/2026-06-18-google-workspace-threatening-to-block-firefox/warning-screen.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercedes-Benz has begun large-scale production of its electric axial flux motor, a topology prized for high power density and compact packaging. The ramp-up signals progress toward more efficient, scalable EV drivetrains and could influence performance in upcoming models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅赛德斯-奔驰已开始大规模生产电轴向磁通电机，这种拓扑在单位体积内提供更高的功率密度且体积更紧凑。此举表明其在实现更高效、可扩展的电动传动系统方面迈出关键一步，可能影响未来车型的性能与成本。</p>
