<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48558018' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mareksuppa.com/til/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TIL: You can make HTTP requests without curl using Bash /dev/TCP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>今天学到：可以用 Bash 的 /dev/TCP 发起 HTTP 请求，无需 curl</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48558018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bash can issue HTTP requests by opening a TCP connection with /dev/tcp and writing raw HTTP, avoiding curl. This technique is handy in minimal environments or quick checks, but it&#x27;s brittle, lacks features like redirects and timeouts, and requires manual response parsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过打开 /dev/tcp 的 TCP 连接并发送原始 HTTP 请求即可在 Bash 中完成网络请求，无需 curl。适合极简环境和快速诊断，但缺乏如重定向、超时等特性，需要手动解析响应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mareksuppa.com/og/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl.png' data-full='https://mareksuppa.com/og/bash-dev-tcp-http-without-curl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620090' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sandimetz.com/blog/2016/1/20/the-wrong-abstraction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prefer duplication over the wrong abstraction (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宁可重复，也别用错的抽象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Duplicating code can keep intent clear when an abstraction would misfit. Premature, poorly fitting abstractions hurt maintainability; the post argues that clarity beats clever architecture and that refactoring can come later when the need is real.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在抽象不合适时，重复实现能保持意图清晰。过早的、与场景不符的抽象会降低可维护性；文章主张清晰胜过花哨的架构，真正需要时再进行重构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' data-full='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science is a new product focused on scientific workloads, offering specialized reasoning and data-handling features for researchers. It aims to speed up literature review, modeling, and experimentation workflows for scientists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science 是面向科学工作负载的新产品，提供专门的推理和数据处理能力，帮助研究人员加速分析与建模。面向研究人员和数据科学家，旨在优化文献综述、实验设计与数据分析的工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48357075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stanford&#x27;s CS336 course teaches language modeling from scratch, focusing on core ideas and practical intuition without heavy dependencies. It&#x27;s useful for researchers and engineers who want to understand model architecture and training dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯坦福 CS336 课程教学生从零开始构建语言模型，强调核心概念与直觉，尽量减少对依赖的要求。对想要理解模型结构与训练动态的研究者和工程师很有价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48599515' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There are no instances in ATProto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ATProto 中不存在实例概念</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATProto’s architecture is often misunderstood as having &#x27;instances&#x27;. The author argues there are no such isolated &#x27;instances&#x27; in ATProto because federation uses accounts and servers differently, emphasizing interoperability and same-identity across servers. This reframes how developers should think about hosting and moderation in ATProto.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATProto 的架构常被误解为‘实例’概念。作者指出在 ATProto 的联邦模型里并不存在真正的‘实例’，强调跨服务器的互操作性和统一身份的重要性，这对开发者在托管与 moderation 上的思路有显著影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/opengraph-image?5b0b970dfd19bb8c' data-full='https://overreacted.io/there-are-no-instances-in-atproto/opengraph-image?5b0b970dfd19bb8c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/mfts0/status/2070080422482977095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hey Nico, you didn&#x27;t vibe code your data room but stole it from Papermark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>嘿 Nico，你没有为数据房写代码却从 Papermark 偷来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accusation that Nico didn’t code his data room but copied it from Papermark, highlighting the importance of provenance and originality in data tooling. The thread serves as a cautionary note on attribution, tooling trust, and the risks of reusing others&#x27; work without proper credit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人指控 Nico 没有自己实现数据房的代码，而是抄袭自 Papermark，这凸显了数据工具中来源证明与署名的重要性。此事强调在工具生态中对原创性和 attribution 的信任风险，以及对他人工作未正确致谢的潜在后果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apertvs.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apertus – Open Foundation Model for Sovereign AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apertus：面向主权 AI 的开放基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apertus unveils an open foundation model designed for sovereign AI deployment with data sovereignty baked in. It supports open weights and configurable safety controls to reduce dependence on centralized providers, enabling in-country hosting and governance. This matters for governments and organizations seeking transparency and autonomy in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apertus 发布一个开放的基础模型，支持主权 AI 的部署与数据自主管控。提供开源权重和可配置的安全策略，旨在降低对大型厂商的依赖，使其能在本地或受监管环境中运行。此举对政府与重视数据主权与透明度的机构尤为重要。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48346947' data-tags='Health,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48346947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. If confirmed, this could influence dietary guidelines and therapeutic strategies for neurodegenerative diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。若再证实，这将影响膳食建议和神经退行性疾病的治疗策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' data-full='http://thesciverse.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/Scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-Alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48403980' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic&#x27;s open-source framework for AI-powered vulnerability discovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 开源框架用于 AI 驱动的漏洞发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48403980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released an open-source framework to aid AI-assisted vulnerability discovery in software. The toolkit aims to standardize how researchers build, run, and compare AI-driven security analyses, promoting reproducible, faster security research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布了一个开源框架，用以辅助 AI 驱动的软件漏洞发现。该工具集旨在标准化研究者如何构建、运行并比较 AI 相关的安全分析，推动可重复的漏洞研究并可能加快修复进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e3145d29b1b19416b94866819bde9eaa223e4d09e14595ba432f1c3abba8d6e/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e3145d29b1b19416b94866819bde9eaa223e4d09e14595ba432f1c3abba8d6e/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pgdog.dev/blog/our-funding-announcement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PgDog is funded and coming to a database near you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PgDog 获得融资，将进入数据库领域的开发者工具市场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48476466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 245</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PgDog has secured funding and plans to bring PostgreSQL-focused tooling to developers. The round signals investor confidence in database tooling that aims to streamline workflows and tooling around Postgres.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PgDog 获得融资，将推出围绕 PostgreSQL 的开发者工具，旨在简化工作流与数据库运维。投资方信心表明数据库工具市场的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pgdog.dev/assets/images/logo2_wide.png?v=bb9c0ef3de' data-full='https://pgdog.dev/assets/images/logo2_wide.png?v=bb9c0ef3de' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arseniyshestakov.com/2026/06/16/apple-is-about-to-make-hide-my-email-useless/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple is about to make Hide My Email useless</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果即将让 Hide My Email 失效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48559935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A forthcoming change could undermine Hide My Email, reducing its ability to shield users’ real addresses when signing up for services. Privacy-focused users may need to adapt or seek alternatives as Apple alters how aliases are exposed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传闻称苹果即将改变 Hide My Email 的工作方式，降低该功能对用户隐私的保护，可能使真实邮箱更易暴露。隐私导向的用户需要适应新的使用方式，或寻找替代方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.hetzner.com/general/infrastructure-and-availability/price-adjustment/#cloud-servers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hetzner Price Adjustment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hetzner 价格调整</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 522</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48540844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 709</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner updates its pricing for cloud servers and related services, affecting cost calculations for small squads and hobby projects. Users should review the new rates and adjust budgets or workflows accordingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hetzner 调整云服务器及相关服务价格，可能影响小型团队和业余项目的成本计算。用户需要审阅新价格并相应调整预算与工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48447062' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.foodwatch.org/en/eu-banned-pesticides-found-in-rice-tea-and-spices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU-banned pesticides found in rice, tea and spices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在米、茶和香料中发现欧盟禁用农药</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48447062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU-banned pesticides were detected in staple foods like rice, tea, and spices, pointing to ongoing gaps in testing in the global supply chain. The finding underscores the challenges of ensuring pesticide safety in food imports and the regulatory implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在米饭、茶、香料等日常食品中检出欧盟禁用农药，暴露供应链检测与进口监管的薄弱环节。这一发现凸显全球食品贸易中确保农药安全的挑战与监管含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.foodwatch.org/fileadmin/_processed_/f/5/csm_foodwatch_logo_150x150_3ca367d925.jpg' data-full='https://www.foodwatch.org/fileadmin/_processed_/f/5/csm_foodwatch_logo_150x150_3ca367d925.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48400842' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/institute/recursive-self-improvement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When AI Builds Itself: Our progress toward recursive self-improvement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 自我构建：向递归自我提升推进的进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48400842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 688</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic outlines progress in recursive self-improvement, a scenario where AI systems iteratively enhance themselves with reduced human input. The discussion touches on safety, alignment, and governance challenges as capabilities scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 概述递归自我提升的进展，即 AI 系统在尽量减少人类介入的情况下迭代自我改进。讨论涉及能力提升带来的安全、对齐与治理挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48600946' data-tags='Legal,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/court-records-should-be-free' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court Records Should Be Free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院记录应免费开放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48600946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF argues that court records should be freely accessible and machine-readable to improve transparency, research, and accountability. The movement pushes for open court data standards and reduces friction for journalists, researchers, and the public.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EFF 呼吁法院记录应免费向公众开放并可机器可读，以提升透明度、研究和问责。倡导建立公开的法院数据标准，降低记者、研究者和公众的使用成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/supreme-court-3b.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/supreme-court-3b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fablepool.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: FablePool – pool money behind a prompt, and Fable builds it in public</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：FablePool——将资金集中投向提示词，公开开发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48496539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FablePool crowdsources funds to back a single prompt and develops it in the open. The project tests whether community funding can accelerate prompt engineering while inviting transparent, real-time feedback from builders and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FablePool 通过众筹资金来支持单一提示词，并公开开发过程。这一做法在测试社区资金是否能加速提示词工程，同时让开发与用户在实时透明的状态下互动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/65697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic, please ship an official Claude Desktop for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic，请为 Linux 发布官方 Claude 桌面客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48434436' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There is a call for an official Linux desktop app for Claude; currently users rely on web or unofficial options, creating cross-platform friction. A native client would improve performance, privacy, and workflow integration for developers and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁为 Claude 发布官方 Linux 桌面应用；目前多数用户依赖网页或非官方方案，跨平台工作流受阻。原生客户端将提升性能、隐私和开发者工作流整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f575add155c168738e5f69e27a6322c95ab0d6961c3ad602ab9e5c969750c40c/anthropics/claude-code/issues/65697' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f575add155c168738e5f69e27a6322c95ab0d6961c3ad602ab9e5c969750c40c/anthropics/claude-code/issues/65697' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

It helps developers pick the right model for on-device or API-backed use cases. The comparison underscores how deployment context drives model choice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 GLM 5.2 与 Opus 的对比，聚焦在准确性、解码、效率与部署场景上的权衡，帮助开发者在本地部署与云端 API 之间做出选择。比较凸显了部署场景对模型选择的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://specification.website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Website Specification</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出对网站进行正式规范的提案，旨在统一网页结构、语义和相关工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48671468' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/06/23/lastpass-notifies-users-of-yet-another-data-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LastPass notifies users of yet another data breach</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LastPass 再次通知用户数据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48671468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48671468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LastPass alerts users to another data breach, underscoring the enduring security risks in credential managers. The update reinforces the case for enabling multi-factor authentication, using device-based security keys, and adopting zero-trust practices for sensitive accounts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LastPass 再次警示用户发生数据泄露，凸显凭证管理工具持续存在的安全风险。此次更新强调开启多因素认证、使用基于设备的安全密钥，以及对敏感账户实行零信任策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2024/03/lastpass-outage.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2024/03/lastpass-outage.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48413500' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rochester.edu/newscenter/what-is-desalination-definition-ocean-water-704732/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New method turns ocean water into drinking water, without waste</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新方法将海水转化为饮用水且实现零废水排放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 507</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48413500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new desalination method converts ocean water into drinking water with no waste. If scalable, it could dramatically reduce the environmental footprint of desalination and broaden access to freshwater.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新型海水淡化方法能够直接生产饮用水且零废水排放。若实现商业化，可能大幅降低淡化过程的环境影响并提升淡水供应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rochester.edu/newscenter/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/fea-what-is-desalination-definition-ocean-water-2026-04-08_Guo_desalination_0064-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://www.rochester.edu/newscenter/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/fea-what-is-desalination-definition-ocean-water-2026-04-08_Guo_desalination_0064-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48608645' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mysk-research/loupe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loupe – A iOS app that raises awareness about what native apps can see</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loupe：一款揭示原生应用可访问数据的 iOS 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 505</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48608645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loupe is an iOS app that visually demonstrates what native apps can access on-device, helping users understand app permissions and data exposure. The project underscores the importance of privacy transparency in mobile ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loupe 是一款用来直观展示原生应用能访问哪些数据的 iOS 应用，帮助用户理解应用权限与数据暴露。该项目凸显了移动生态系统中隐私透明度的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2baf64109f9489db61acbd445290b098b62ed91c8e4e7579835a356b629fe7e1/mysk-research/loupe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2baf64109f9489db61acbd445290b098b62ed91c8e4e7579835a356b629fe7e1/mysk-research/loupe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
